Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chronax是一个基于JAX的原生时间序列预测库，通过函数式编程范式重构预测系统，解决传统Python数值栈在可组合性、大规模并行化和现代可微分工作流集成方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测库依赖于解释器驱动执行和面向对象抽象，限制了可组合性、大规模并行化以及与现代化可微分和加速器导向工作流的集成。随着预测任务涉及大规模异构时间序列数据、不规则协变量和频繁重训练，对可扩展性和执行效率提出了新需求。

Method: 基于JAX的函数式编程范式，将预处理、建模和多步预测表示为纯JAX函数，利用即时编译和自动向量化等程序变换，实现端到端优化，支持跨CPU、GPU和TPU的加速器就绪执行。

Result: Chronax实现了可扩展的多序列预测、模型无关的符合性不确定性量化，并能够无缝集成到现代机器学习和科学计算流程中。

Conclusion: Chronax通过将JAX的函数式编程范式引入时间序列预测系统设计，为大规模异构时间序列预测提供了新的解决方案，解决了传统预测库在可组合性、并行化和现代工作流集成方面的局限性。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting is central to many scientific and industrial domains, such as energy systems, climate modeling, finance, and retail. While forecasting methods have evolved from classical statistical models to automated, and neural approaches, the surrounding software ecosystem remains anchored to the traditional Python numerical stack. Existing libraries rely on interpreter-driven execution and object-oriented abstractions, limiting composability, large-scale parallelism, and integration with modern differentiable and accelerator-oriented workflows. Meanwhile, today's forecasting increasingly involves large collections of heterogeneous time series data, irregular covariates, and frequent retraining, placing new demands on scalability and execution efficiency. JAX offers an alternative paradigm to traditional stateful numerical computation frameworks based on pure functions and program transformations such as just-in-time compilation and automatic vectorization, enabling end-to-end optimization across CPUs, GPUs, and TPUs. However, this modern paradigm has not yet been fully incorporated into the design of forecasting systems. We introduce Chronax, a JAX-native time-series forecasting library that rethinks forecasting abstractions around functional purity, composable transformations, and accelerator-ready execution. By representing preprocessing, modeling, and multi-horizon prediction as pure JAX functions, Chronax enables scalable multi-series forecasting, model-agnostic conformal uncertainty quantification, and seamless integration with modern machine learning and scientific computing pipelines.

</details>


### [39] [Late Fusion Neural Operators for Extrapolation Across Parameter Space in Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16721)
*Eva van Tegelen,Taniya Kapoor,George A. K. van Voorn,Peter van Heijster,Ioannis N. Athanasiadis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Late Fusion Neural Operator架构，通过解耦状态动态学习和参数效应，在四个PDE基准测试中显著超越现有方法，实现72.9%域内和71.8%域外RMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，物理参数变化导致训练和预测机制之间的分布偏移，使外推成为核心挑战。神经算子中参数整合方式对其泛化能力至关重要，特别是当状态和参数表示纠缠时。

Method: 提出Late Fusion Neural Operator架构，将学习潜在状态表示的神经算子与稀疏回归相结合，以结构化方式整合参数信息，解耦状态动态学习与参数效应。

Result: 在包括平流、Burgers以及1D和2D反应-扩散方程的四个基准PDE测试中，该方法始终优于Fourier Neural Operator和CAPE-FNO。在所有实验中均取得最佳性能，与次优方法相比，平均RMSE降低72.9%（域内）和71.8%（域外）。

Conclusion: Late Fusion Neural Operators在域内和域外参数机制中均表现出强大的泛化能力，通过解耦状态动态学习和参数效应，显著提高了预测性能。

Abstract: Developing neural operators that accurately predict the behavior of systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) across unseen parameter regimes is crucial for robust generalization in scientific and engineering applications. In practical applications, variations in physical parameters induce distribution shifts between training and prediction regimes, making extrapolation a central challenge. As a result, the way parameters are incorporated into neural operator models plays a key role in their ability to generalize, particularly when state and parameter representations are entangled. In this work, we introduce the Late Fusion Neural Operator, an architecture that disentangles learning state dynamics from parameter effects, improving predictive performance both within and beyond the training distribution. Our approach combines neural operators for learning latent state representations with sparse regression to incorporate parameter information in a structured manner. Across four benchmark PDEs including advection, Burgers, and both 1D and 2D reaction-diffusion equations, the proposed method consistently outperforms Fourier Neural Operator and CAPE-FNO. Late Fusion Neural Operators achieve consistently the best performance in all experiments, with an average RMSE reduction of 72.9% in-domain and 71.8% out-domain compared to the second-best method. These results demonstrate strong generalization across both in-domain and out-domain parameter regimes.

</details>


### [40] [Neuroscience Inspired Graph Operators Towards Edge-Deployable Virtual Sensing for Irregular Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16722)
*William Howes,Farid Ahmed,Kazuma Kobayashi,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VS-GNO是一种用于边缘部署的节能神经算子，通过可变脉冲神经元和谱-空间卷积分析，在稀疏到密集的虚拟传感中实现低误差与低功耗平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程系统的实时虚拟传感需要稀疏到密集重建、复杂多物理场、高度不规则几何结构，同时面临严格的延迟和能耗约束。现有神经算子架构很少明确考虑功耗问题，而脉冲神经元集成在神经形态硬件上虽能降低功耗，但现有模型在回归型虚拟传感中性能严重下降。

Method: 提出可变脉冲图神经算子(VS-GNO)，集成复杂的谱-空间卷积分析和先前开发的可变脉冲神经元(VSN)，采用能量-误差平衡损失函数。VS-GNO包含谱卷积和空间卷积两种形式。

Result: 非脉冲L2误差基线为0.4%。VS-GNO谱卷积形式实现0.71%重建误差，平均脉冲率为15%；完整形式实现1.04%重建误差，脉冲率为24.5%。在保持较低误差的同时显著降低能耗。

Conclusion: VS-GNO是向能量高效、边缘可部署神经算子迈出的有希望一步，适用于复杂、高度不规则工程环境中的实时稀疏到密集虚拟传感。

Abstract: Predicting full-field physics through the real-time virtual sensing of engineering systems can enhance limited physical sensors but often requires sparse-to-dense reconstruction, complex multiphysics, and highly irregular geometries as well as strict latency and energy constraints for edge-deployability. Neural operators have been presented as a potential candidate for such applications but few architectures exist that explicitly address power consumption. Spiking neuron integration can provide a potential solution when integrated on neuromorphic hardware but the current existing neuron models result in severe performance degradation towards regression-based virtual sensing. To address the performance concerns and edge-constraints, we present the Variable Spiking Graph Neural Operator (VS-GNO) which integrates a sophisticated spectral-spatial convolutional analysis and a previously developed Variable Spiking Neuron (VSN) and energy-error balance loss function. With a non-spiking $L_2$ error baseline of $0.4\%$, VS-GNO can provide a reconstruction error of $0.71\%$ with $15\%$ average spiking in its spectral-only form and $1.04\%$ with $24.5\%$ spiking in its entire form. These results position VS-GNO as a promising step towards energy-efficient, edge-deployable neural operators for real-time sparse-to-dense virtual sensing in complex, highly irregular engineering environments.

</details>


### [41] [LLM-Extracted Covariates for Clinical Causal Inference: Rethinking Integration Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16763)
*Lei Liu,Jialin Chen,Kathy Macropol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM从电子病历文本中提取潜在混杂变量，结合倾向评分模型可显著改善因果推断效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子病历中的关键临床状态（如衰弱、治疗目标、精神状态）仅记录在自由文本中，导致未测量混杂问题，限制了因果推断的准确性。大语言模型能够从文本中提取这些潜在混杂变量，但如何有效整合到因果估计流程中尚未系统研究。

Method: 使用MIMIC-IV数据库中21,859名脓毒症患者，比较七种协变量整合策略：表格数据基线、传统NLP表示、三种LLM增强方法。重点比较了直接增强倾向评分模型与基于文本分类距离的双卡钳匹配等方法。

Result: 在已知真实效应的半合成实验中，LLM增强的倾向评分将估计偏差从0.0143降至0.0003。在真实数据中，整合LLM提取的协变量将估计的治疗效应从0.055降至0.027，与CLOVERS随机试验方向一致。双重稳健估计器得到0.019，证实了结果的稳健性。

Conclusion: 直接增强倾向评分模型是整合文本衍生协变量的最有效策略，而基于文本分类距离的双卡钳匹配会限制供体池并降低估计质量。研究为在重症监护中何时以及如何使用文本衍生协变量改善因果估计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Causal inference from electronic health records (EHR) is fundamentally limited by unmeasured confounding: critical clinical states such as frailty, goals of care, and mental status are documented in free-text notes but absent from structured data. Large language models can extract these latent confounders as interpretable, structured covariates, yet how to effectively integrate them into causal estimation pipelines has not been systematically studied. Using the MIMIC-IV database with 21,859 sepsis patients, we compare seven covariate-integration strategies for estimating the effect of early vasopressor initiation on 28-day mortality, spanning tabular-only baselines, traditional NLP representations, and three LLM-augmented approaches. A central finding is that not all integration strategies are equally effective: directly augmenting the propensity score model with LLM covariates achieves the best performance, while dual-caliper matching on text-derived categorical distances restricts the donor pool and degrades estimation. In semi-synthetic experiments with known ground-truth effects, LLM-augmented propensity scores reduce estimation bias from 0.0143 to 0.0003 relative to tabular-only methods, and this advantage persists under substantial simulated extraction error. On real data, incorporating LLM-extracted covariates reduces the estimated treatment effect from 0.055 to 0.027, directionally consistent with the CLOVERS randomized trial, and a doubly robust estimator yielding 0.019 confirms the robustness of this finding. Our results offer practical guidance on when and how text-derived covariates improve causal estimation in critical care.

</details>


### [42] [Representation Before Training: A Fixed-Budget Benchmark for Generative Medical Event Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16775)
*Inhyeok Lee,Luke Solo,Michael C. Burkhart,Bashar Ramadan,William F. Parker,Brett K. Beaulieu-Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了医疗事件生成模型中输入表示方法对下游预测性能的影响，通过三个实验比较了28个匹配的transformer模型在MIMIC-IV数据集上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式医疗事件模型的预测性能受临床事件标记化方式的影响，但输入表示通常与其他系统和架构选择混在一起，很少被独立评估。本研究旨在隔离表示决策的影响，评估在共享的单轮预训练预算下，不同表示方法如何影响下游预测性能。

Method: 在MIMIC-IV数据集上训练28个匹配的transformer模型，评估30个临床结局。三个实验包括：(1)量化粒度、参考范围锚定和代码-值融合；(2)值编码（硬分箱、软离散化、代码归一化xVal）与时序编码（事件顺序、时间标记、入院相对RoPE）的交叉；(3)原生MIMIC实验室/生命体征代码与CLIF重映射代码的比较，包括压缩保持扰动。

Result: 代码-值融合标记化将死亡率AUROC从0.891提升至0.915，住院时长AUROC从0.763提升至0.788，13个回归结局的平均Spearman rho从0.414提升至0.494。在三种时序编码中，仅事件顺序和入院相对RoPE平均表现与插入时间标记相当或更好，同时序列长度缩短11%。CLIF重映射在单中心设置中保持性能，同时产生更小、临床可解释的标记集。更细的量化、参考范围锚定和软离散化在特定结局中有帮助，而代码归一化xVal表现较差。

Conclusion: 输入表示选择对医疗事件模型的预测性能有显著影响。代码-值融合标记化能显著提升性能，时序编码的简化方法（仅事件顺序或入院相对RoPE）在保持性能的同时缩短序列。CLIF重映射为多中心使用提供了兼容性。这些发现强调了在医疗AI模型开发中独立评估和优化输入表示的重要性。

Abstract: Every prediction from a generative medical event model is bounded by how clinical events are tokenized, yet input representation is rarely isolated from other system and architectural choices. We evaluate how representation decisions affect downstream prediction after a shared one-epoch pretraining budget. We train 28 matched transformers on MIMIC-IV and evaluate them on 30 clinical outcomes in three experiments: (1) quantization granularity, reference-range anchoring, and code-value fusion; (2) value encoding (hard bins, soft discretization, code-normalized xVal) crossed with temporal encoding (event order, time tokens, admission-relative RoPE); and (3) native MIMIC laboratory/vital codes versus the Common Longitudinal ICU Format (CLIF)-remapped laboratory/vital codes with compression-preserving perturbation arms. In Experiment 1, fused code-value tokenization improves mortality AUROC from 0.891 to 0.915 (BH-adjusted p < 0.001), hospital length-of-stay AUROC from 0.763 to 0.788 (BH-adjusted p < 0.001), and, for the decile fused-vs-unfused comparison, mean regression Spearman rho across the 13 regression outcomes from 0.414 to 0.494. Across the three temporal encodings, event order only and admission-relative RoPE match or exceed inserting time tokens on average while shortening sequences by 11%. CLIF remapping preserves downstream performance in our single-site setting while yielding a smaller, clinically interpretable token set compatible with multi-site use. Finer-than-decile quantization, reference-range anchoring, and soft discretization help in selective outcomes, while code-normalized xVal remains well below the discrete and soft families, consistent with near-median suppression that persists after the affine variant.

</details>


### [43] [Continuous Limits of Coupled Flows in Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16801)
*Zilin Li,Weiwei Xu,Xuchun Tong,Xuanbo Lu,Xuanqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将去中心化学习形式化为黎曼流形上的耦合慢-快动力系统，证明了离散空间转移收敛于过阻尼朗之万SDE，表示权重无条件避免发散并与空间测度的主特征空间对齐，最终在平稳极限处自发出现正交解耦、线性可分的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代表示学习严重依赖全局误差信号，而去中心化算法提供了一种分布式替代方案。然而，这些离散动力学在连续数据流形上的宏观收敛特性在理论上尚未解决，且存在参数爆炸问题。本文旨在弥合这一理论空白。

Method: 1. 将去中心化学习形式化为黎曼流形上的耦合慢-快动力系统；2. 使用测度理论极限证明离散空间转移一致收敛于过阻尼朗之万随机微分方程；3. 通过伊藤-泊松解子和LaSalle不变原理的随机扩展，建立表示权重的收敛性；4. 构造完全耦合的空间-参数流的联合李雅普诺夫泛函。

Result: 1. 证明了离散空间转移一致收敛于过阻尼朗之万SDE；2. 表示权重无条件避免发散，并与空间测度的主特征空间严格对齐；3. 证明了全局耗散性；4. 在平稳极限处自发出现正交解耦、线性可分的特征。

Conclusion: 该框架将离散算法与连续随机分析联系起来，为去中心化表示学习提供了正式的理论基准，证明了去中心化学习能够自发产生结构良好的表示特征。

Abstract: While modern representation learning relies heavily on global error signals, decentralized algorithms driven by local interactions offer a fundamental distributed alternative. However, the macroscopic convergence properties of these discrete dynamics on continuous data manifolds remain theoretically unresolved, notoriously suffering from parameter explosion. We bridge this gap by formalizing decentralized learning as a coupled slow-fast dynamical system on Riemannian manifolds. First, using measure-theoretic limits, we prove that the discrete spatial transitions converge uniformly to an overdamped Langevin stochastic differential equation. Second, via the Itô-Poisson resolvent and a stochastic extension of LaSalle's Invariance Principle, we establish that the representation weights unconditionally avoid divergence and align strictly with the principal eigenspace of the spatial measure. Finally, we construct a joint Lyapunov functional for the fully coupled spatial-parametric flow. This proves global dissipativity and demonstrates that orthogonally disentangled, linearly separable features emerge spontaneously at the stationary limit. Our framework bridges discrete algorithms with continuous stochastic analysis, providing a formal theoretical baseline for decentralized representation learning.

</details>


### [44] [AutoOR: Scalably Post-training LLMs to Autoformalize Operations Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16804)
*Sumeet Ramesh Motwani,Chuan Du,Aleksander Petrov,Christopher Davis,Philip Torr,Antonio Papania-Davis,Weishi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoOR是一个基于合成数据生成和强化学习的管道，用于训练LLMs将自然语言描述的优化问题自动形式化为求解器可处理的数学公式，在多个OR基准测试中达到SOTA或竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业决策中的优化问题需要专业的运筹学知识才能转化为求解器可处理的数学公式，这一过程难以规模化。现有方法无法有效处理从自然语言到数学形式的自动转换。

Method: AutoOR采用可扩展的合成数据生成和强化学习管道：1）从标准优化形式生成经过验证的训练数据；2）使用求解器执行反馈作为强化学习的奖励信号；3）针对非线性问题类引入课程RL策略，从有限的初始训练数据引导学习。

Result: 在六个已建立的OR基准测试中，AutoOR应用于8B模型取得了SOTA或竞争性结果，匹配了显著更大的前沿模型。对于涉及物理动力学的非线性问题类（前沿模型得分接近0%），课程RL策略使该问题类变得可处理。

Conclusion: AutoOR等方法可以显著加速工业决策中的AI应用，通过自动形式化优化问题降低专业门槛，提高可扩展性。

Abstract: Optimization problems are central to decision-making in manufacturing, logistics, scheduling, and other industrial settings. Translating complicated descriptions of these problems into solver-ready formulations requires specialized operations research (OR) expertise, making it hard to scale. We present AutoOR, a scalable synthetic data generation and reinforcement learning pipeline that trains LLMs to autoformalize optimization problems specified in natural language across linear, mixed-integer, and non-linear categories. AutoOR generates verified training data from standard optimization forms and uses solver execution feedback as the reward signal for RL post-training. AutoOR applied to an 8B model achieves state-of-the-art or competitive results across six established OR benchmarks, matching significantly larger frontier models. For a non-linear problem class involving physical dynamics, where frontier models score near 0%, we introduce a curriculum RL strategy that bootstraps from limited initial training data to make this class tractable for post-training. We believe that methods such as AutoOR can significantly accelerate industrial decision-making with AI.

</details>


### [45] [Self-Reinforcing Controllable Synthesis of Rare Relational Data via Bayesian Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16817)
*Chongsheng Zhang,Hao Wang,Zelong Yu,Esteban Garces Arias,Julian Rodemann,Zhanshuo Zhang,Qilong Li,Gaojuan Fan,Krikamol Muandet,Christian Heumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RDDG是一个基于LLM的关系数据生成框架，通过动态指导和自增强反馈机制优化不平衡分类任务的表格数据合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中普遍存在数据不平衡问题，LLM在文本生成方面表现出色，但在关系型/结构化表格数据合成方面的应用尚未充分探索。现有方法缺乏有效的反馈机制来指导LLM在合成过程中持续优化生成数据质量。

Method: 提出RDDG框架：1) 使用核心集选择从原始数据中识别代表性样本；2) 利用上下文学习发现核心集中属性间的内在模式和相关性；3) 在保持上述约束条件下生成表格数据；4) 引入自增强反馈机制，对生成数据质量进行自动评估，实现生成过程中的持续质量优化。

Result: 在多个真实和合成数据集上的实验结果表明，RDDG在数据保真度和下游不平衡分类性能方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RDDG是一个统一的上下文学习框架，通过渐进式思维链步骤和动态指导机制，能够有效生成高质量的表格数据以提升下游不平衡分类性能，为LLM在关系数据合成领域的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Imbalanced data is commonly present in real-world applications. While data synthesis can effectively mitigate the data scarcity problem of rare-classes, and LLMs have revolutionized text generation, the application of LLMs to relational/structured tabular data synthesis remains underexplored. Moreover, existing approaches lack an effective feedback mechanism that can guide LLMs towards continuously optimizing the quality of the generated data throughout the synthesis process. In this work, we propose RDDG, Relational Data generator with Dynamic Guidance, which is a unified in-context learning framework that employs progressive chain-of-thought (CoT) steps to generate tabular data for enhancing downstream imbalanced classification performance. RDDG first uses core set selection to identify representative samples from the original data, then utilizes in-context learning to discover the inherent patterns and correlations among attributes within the core set, and subsequently generates tabular data while preserving the aforementioned constraints. More importantly, it incorporates a self-reinforcing feedback mechanism that provides automatic assessments on the quality of the generated data, enabling continuous quality optimization throughout the generation process. Experimental results on multiple real and synthetic datasets demonstrate that RDDG outperforms existing approaches in both data fidelity and downstream imbalanced classification performance. We make our code available at https://github.com/cszhangLMU/RDDG.

</details>


### [46] [R&F-Inventory: A Large-Scale Dataset for Monotonic Inventory Estimation in Reach and Frequency Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16821)
*Yunshan Peng,Ji Wu,Wentao Bai,Yunke Bai,Jinan Pang,Wenzheng Shu,Yanxiang Zeng,Xialong Liu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并发布了一个大规模R&F合约库存预估数据集，该数据集以"定向-排期-频控"为基本上下文，提供同一上下文中多个预算点对应的UV和PV观测值，形成完整的预算-性能曲线，支持R&F合约广告研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开广告数据集大多基于独立样本，缺乏对R&F合约中"预算-性能曲线"（包括UV和PV）核心结构的刻画。广告主在创建R&F合约时需要实时查看不同预算水平下的UV、PV变化曲线，但现有数据无法满足这一需求。

Method: 构建大规模R&F合约库存预估数据集，以"定向-排期-频控"为基本上下文，提供同一上下文中多个预算点对应的UV和PV观测值，形成完整的预算-性能曲线。数据集明确包含基于时间窗口的频控机制，并满足预算和排期维度的单调性和边际收益递减特性。推导理论最大曝光天花板作为一致性检验标准。

Result: 创建了包含完整预算-性能曲线的大规模R&F合约数据集，定义了单点性能预测和预算-性能曲线重建两个标准化基准任务，提供可复现的基线方法和评估协议。数据集支持结构约束学习、单调回归、曲线一致性建模和R&F合约规划等问题的系统研究。

Conclusion: 提出的R&F合约库存预估数据集填补了现有广告数据集的空白，为R&F合约广告研究提供了标准化基准，支持广告主在创建合约时实时评估不同预算水平下的性能变化，推动了品牌广告领域的系统性研究。

Abstract: Reach and Frequency (R&F) contract advertising is an important form of widely used brand advertising. Unlike performance advertising, R&F contracts emphasize controllable delivery of UV and PV under given targeting, scheduling, and frequency control constraints. In practical systems, advertisers typically need to view the UV, PV change curves at different budget levels in real time when creating an R&F contract. However, most existing publicly available advertising datasets are based on independent samples, lacking a characterization of the core structure of the "budget-performance curve" (including UV and PV) in R&F contracts.This paper proposes and releases a large-scale R&F contract inventory estimation dataset. This dataset uses the R&F contract context consisting of "targeting-scheduling-frequency control" as the basic context, providing observations of UV and PV corresponding to multiple budget points within the same context, thus forming a complete budget-performance curve. The dataset explicitly includes a time-window-based frequency control mechanism (e.g.,"no more than 3 times within 5 days") and naturally satisfies the monotonicity and diminishing marginal returns characteristics in the budget and scheduling dimensions. We further derive the theoretical maximum exposure ceiling and use it as a consistency check to evaluate data quality and the feasibility of model predictions. Using this data set, this paper defines two standardized benchmark tasks: single-point performance prediction and reconstruction of budget-performance curves, and provides a set of reproducible baseline methods and evaluation protocols. This dataset can support systematic research on problems such as structural constraint learning, monotonic regression, curve consistency modeling, and R&F contract planning.The code for our experiments can be found at https://github.com/pengyunshan/RF-Inventory.

</details>


### [47] [The Illusion of Certainty: Decoupling Capability and Calibration in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16830)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Xiangyu Peng,Qinglin Chen,Qinyuan Ye,Caiming Xiong,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CaOPD提出了一种校准感知的在线策略蒸馏框架，解决传统OPD导致的模型过度自信问题，通过基于模型rollouts的实证置信度估计实现帕累托最优校准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现在线策略蒸馏虽然能有效提升任务准确率，但会系统性地导致模型过度自信，这源于训练时教师监督基于特权上下文信息，而部署时模型只能使用部署时信息，存在信息不匹配问题

Method: 提出CaOPD框架：1)从模型rollouts估计实证置信度；2)用学生基础目标替换自报告的置信度；3)通过相同的自蒸馏流程蒸馏修订后的响应

Result: 实验表明CaOPD在各种模型和领域上实现了帕累托最优校准，同时保持竞争力，在分布外和持续学习场景下具有鲁棒泛化能力

Conclusion: 能力蒸馏并不意味着校准的置信度，置信度应被视为后训练中的重要目标，CaOPD为解决OPD中的校准问题提供了有效框架

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is an increasingly important paradigm for post-training language models. However, we identify a pervasive Scaling Law of Miscalibration: while OPD effectively improves task accuracy, it systematically traps models in severe overconfidence. We trace this failure to an information mismatch: teacher supervision is formed under privileged context available during training, whereas the deployed model must report confidence using only deployment-time information. We formalize this perspective theoretically, showing that teacher-conditioned success is generally not a valid target for deployment-time confidence and that helpful privileged context induces entropy collapse and a systematic optimism bias. To address this, we propose a calibration-aware OPD framework, CaOPD, that estimates empirical confidence from model rollouts, replaces self-reported confidence with this student-grounded target, and distills the revised response through the same self-distillation pipeline. Experiments across various models and domains show that CaOPD achieves Pareto-optimal calibration while maintaining competitive capability, generalizing robustly under out-of-distribution and continual learning. Our findings highlight that capability distillation does not imply calibrated confidence, and that confidence should be treated as an essential objective in post-training. Code: https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/CaOPD

</details>


### [48] [Applications of deep generative models to DNA reaction kinetics and to cryogenic electron microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16851)
*Chenwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了深度生成模型如何通过整合领域知识与深度学习来推进生物问题分析，重点关注DNA反应动力学和冷冻电镜两个领域，提出了多种创新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生物问题分析中的挑战，特别是DNA反应动力学模拟结果的可解释性和冷冻电镜密度图分析中的关键问题，通过整合领域知识与深度学习来提高分析效率和准确性。

Method: 1. DNA反应动力学：提出ViDa框架，结合变分自编码器（VAEs）和几何散射变换生成生物物理合理的DNA反应动力学模拟嵌入，降维到二维空间可视化反应路径。
2. 冷冻电镜分析：首先对原子模型构建的深度学习方法进行全面综述和基准测试；然后提出Struc2mapGAN，从蛋白质结构生成高保真冷冻电镜密度图；最后提出CryoSAMU，通过跨注意力机制整合密度特征和蛋白质语言模型的结构嵌入来增强中等分辨率冷冻电镜图。

Result: ViDa能够保持结构并聚类轨迹集合到反应路径，使模拟结果更易解释并揭示新的机制见解。Struc2mapGAN成功合成实验级高保真冷冻电镜密度图。CryoSAMU有效增强中等分辨率冷冻电镜图质量。整体展示了深度生成模型在解释DNA反应机制和推进冷冻电镜分析方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 深度生成模型在整合领域知识与深度学习方面具有巨大潜力，能够显著提升DNA反应动力学分析和冷冻电镜密度图解释的能力，为生物问题分析提供了新的有效工具和方法论。

Abstract: This dissertation explores how deep generative models can advance the analysis of challenging biological problems by integrating domain knowledge with deep learning. It focuses on two areas: DNA reaction kinetics and cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM). In the first part, we present ViDa, a biophysics-informed framework leveraging variational autoencoders (VAEs) and geometric scattering transforms to generate biophysically-plausible embeddings of DNA reaction kinetics simulations. These embeddings are reduced to a two-dimensional space to visualize DNA hybridization and toehold-mediated strand displacement reactions. ViDa preserves structure and clusters trajectory ensembles into reaction pathways, making simulation results more interpretable and revealing new mechanistic insights. In the second part, we address key challenges in cryo-EM density map interpretation and protein structure modeling. We provide a comprehensive review and benchmarking of deep learning methods for atomic model building, with improved evaluation metrics and practical guidance. We then present Struc2mapGAN, a generative adversarial network that synthesizes high-fidelity experimental-like cryo-EM density maps from protein structures. Finally, we present CryoSAMU, a structure-aware multimodal U-Net that enhances intermediate-resolution cryo-EM maps by integrating density features with structural embeddings from protein language models via cross-attention. Overall, these contributions demonstrate the potential of deep generative models to interpret DNA reaction mechanisms and advance cryo-EM density map analysis and protein structure modeling.

</details>


### [49] [CCAR: Intrinsic Robustness as an Emergent Geometric Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16861)
*Akash Samanta,Manish Pratap Singh,Debasis Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CCAR方法通过类条件激活正则化在特征空间施加块对角结构，将不同类别的能量限制在正交子空间中，从而增强表示几何结构并提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督学习只关注预测精度，忽略了学习特征的内部几何结构，导致表示纠缠且脆弱。需要显式地设计特征空间几何结构来提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出类条件激活正则化(CCAR)，通过软归纳偏置在特征空间施加块对角结构，将不同类别的激活能量限制在正交子空间中，形成几何支架。

Result: 理论分析表明该方法与Fisher判别比最大化相关，建立了几何解纠缠与算法稳定性间的形式化联系。在标签噪声和输入损坏基准测试中显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性是良好设计的特征空间的涌现属性，通过显式工程化特征几何结构可以自然过滤噪声和对抗扰动，实现更好的算法稳定性。

Abstract: Standard supervised learning optimizes for predictive accuracy but remains agnostic to the internal geometry of learned features, often yielding representations that are entangled and brittle. We propose Class-Conditional Activation Regularization (CCAR) to explicitly engineer the feature space, imposing a block-diagonal structure via a soft inductive bias. By shaping the latent representation to confine class energy to orthogonal subspaces, we create an intrinsic geometric scaffold that naturally filters noise and adversarial perturbations. We provide theoretical analysis linking this structural constraint to the maximization of the Fisher Discriminant Ratio, establishing a formal connection between geometric disentanglement and algorithmic stability. Empirically, this approach demonstrates that robustness is an emergent property of a well-engineered feature space, significantly outperforming baselines on label noise and input corruption benchmarks.

</details>


### [50] [OC-Distill: Ontology-aware Contrastive Learning with Cross-Modal Distillation for ICU Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16878)
*Zhongyuan Liang,Junhyung Jo,Hyang-Jung Lee,Sang Kyu Kim,Irene Y. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OC-Distill：一种两阶段框架，通过本体感知对比学习和跨模态知识蒸馏，利用多模态监督训练，仅需生命体征即可在推理时实现ICU患者严重临床恶化和住院时间预测的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU风险预测方法存在两个主要局限：1）对比预训练将所有患者视为同等强度的负样本，未能捕捉具有相关诊断患者间的临床相似性；2）下游微调通常忽略临床记录等补充模态，这些模态提供了生理信号无法单独提供的丰富上下文信息。

Method: 提出OC-Distill两阶段框架：第一阶段引入本体感知对比目标，利用ICD层次结构量化患者相似性并学习临床基础表示；第二阶段通过跨模态知识蒸馏微调预训练编码器，将临床记录中的补充信息转移到模型中。训练时利用多模态监督，推理时仅需生命体征。

Result: 在MIMIC数据集上的多个ICU预测任务中，OC-Distill展示了改进的标签效率，并在仅使用生命体征进行推理的方法中实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: OC-Distill通过结合本体感知对比学习和跨模态知识蒸馏，有效解决了现有ICU风险预测方法的局限性，实现了更好的临床表示学习和预测性能，同时保持了推理时的实用性（仅需生命体征）。

Abstract: Early prediction of severe clinical deterioration and remaining length of stay can enable timely intervention and better resource allocation in high-acuity settings such as the ICU. This has driven the development of machine learning models that leverage continuous streams of vital signs and other physiological signals for real-time risk prediction. Despite their promise, existing methods have important limitations. Contrastive pretraining treats all patients as equally strong negatives, failing to capture clinically meaningful similarity between patients with related diagnoses. Meanwhile, downstream fine-tuning typically ignores complementary modalities such as clinical notes, which provide rich contextual information unavailable in physiological signals alone. To address these challenges, we propose OC-Distill, a two-stage framework that leverages multimodal supervision during training while requiring only vital signs at inference. In the first stage, we introduce an ontology-aware contrastive objective that exploits the ICD hierarchy to quantify patient similarity and learn clinically grounded representations. In the second stage, we fine-tune the pretrained encoder via cross-modal knowledge distillation, transferring complementary information from clinical notes into the model. Across multiple ICU prediction tasks on MIMIC, OC-Distill demonstrates improved label efficiency and achieves state-of-the-art performance among methods that use only vital signs at inference.

</details>


### [51] [SinkRouter: Sink-Aware Routing for Efficient Long-Context Decoding in Large Language and Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16883)
*Junnan Liu,Xinyan Liu,Peifeng Gao,Zhaobo Qi,Beichen Zhang,Weigang Zhang,Antoni Bert Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SinkRouter：一种基于注意力下沉现象机制理解的无训练选择性路由框架，通过检测下沉信号跳过近零输出计算，在保持精度的同时显著提升长上下文解码效率，最高实现2.03倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文解码加速方法存在两个主要问题：1）依赖启发式剪枝，可能丢弃有用信息，以效率换取精度；2）缺乏对注意力下沉现象的机制理解，通常不加区分地保留高分token、将早期token视为必需锚点或依赖启发式头路由。本文旨在基于对注意力下沉现象作为稳定、可达、误差可控固定点的理解，开发更有效的加速方案。

Method: 提出SinkRouter框架：1）基于注意力下沉现象对应训练中构建的稳定、可达、误差可控固定点的理论洞察；2）无训练选择性路由机制，检测下沉信号并跳过产生近零输出的计算；3）硬件感知的Triton内核实现，采用块级分支和Split-K并行化技术。

Result: 在多样化长上下文基准测试（LongBench、InfiniteBench、CVBench、MileBench、MMVP）上，使用文本和多模态骨干模型（Llama-3.1-8B/70B、Yi-9B-200K、LLaVA-1.5-7B/13B）进行广泛评估。SinkRouter在保持竞争性精度的同时，一致提升解码效率，在512K上下文长度下达到2.03倍加速。

Conclusion: SinkRouter通过机制性地理解注意力下沉现象，实现了训练自由的选择性路由，在多样化长上下文任务和模型上实现了显著的解码加速，同时保持了模型精度，为长上下文解码的效率-精度权衡提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In long-context decoding for LLMs and LMMs, attention becomes increasingly memory-bound because each decoding step must load a large amount of KV-cache data from GPU memory. Existing acceleration strategies often trade efficiency for accuracy by relying on heuristic pruning that may discard useful information. At a deeper level, they also tend to indiscriminately preserve all high-scoring tokens, treat early tokens as indispensable anchors, or rely on heuristic head routing, reflecting an insufficient mechanistic understanding of the attention sink phenomenon. In this paper, we show that the attention sink phenomenon corresponds to a stable, reachable, and error-controllable fixed point constructed during training. Based on this insight, we propose SinkRouter, a training-free selective routing framework that detects the sink signal and skips computations that would otherwise produce near-zero output. To translate this mechanism into real-world acceleration, we develop a hardware-aware Triton kernel with block-level branching and Split-K parallelism. We conduct extensive evaluations on a diverse suite of long-context benchmarks, including LongBench, InfiniteBench, CVBench, MileBench, and MMVP, using both text-only and multimodal backbones such as Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.1-70B, Yi-9B-200K, LLaVA-1.5-7B, and LLaVA-1.5-13B. Across these settings, SinkRouter consistently improves decoding efficiency while maintaining competitive accuracy, and reaches 2.03x speedup with a 512K context.

</details>


### [52] [Towards Fully Parameter-Free Stochastic Optimization: Grid Search with Self-Bounding Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16888)
*Yuheng Zhao,Yu-Hu Yan,Amit Attia,Tomer Koren,Lijun Zhang,Peng Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRASP框架实现完全参数自由的随机优化，通过自边界分析技术自动确定参数搜索范围，在非凸和凸优化中均取得竞争性收敛速率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数自由方法仍依赖问题参数的先验知识（如上下界），属于"部分参数自由"。本文旨在实现"完全参数自由"方法，即算法输入不需要满足任何与真实问题参数相关的不可验证条件

Method: 提出GRASP网格搜索框架，采用新颖的自边界分析技术自动确定参数搜索范围。框架包含模型集成作为最终步骤，在插值方差特征下提供更精确的保证

Result: 在非凸情况下，提出的完全参数自由方法达到接近最优的收敛速率（对数因子内）；在凸情况下，参数自由方法在加速和普适性方面具有竞争性性能

Conclusion: GRASP框架通过自边界分析实现了真正完全参数自由的随机优化，在非凸和凸优化中均表现出色，为参数自由优化提供了通用解决方案

Abstract: Parameter-free stochastic optimization aims to design algorithms that are agnostic to the underlying problem parameters while still achieving convergence rates competitive with optimally tuned methods. While some parameter-free methods do not require the specific values of the problem parameters, they still rely on prior knowledge, such as the lower or upper bounds of them. We refer to such methods as ``partially parameter-free''. In this work, we target achieving ``fully parameter-free'' methods, i.e., the algorithmic inputs do not need to satisfy any unverifiable condition related to the true problem parameters. We propose a powerful and general grid search framework, named \textsc{Grasp}, with a novel self-bounding analysis technique that effectively determines the search ranges of parameters, in contrast to previous work. Our method demonstrates generality in: (i) the non-convex case, where we propose a fully parameter-free method that achieves near-optimal convergence rate, up to logarithmic factors; (ii) the convex case, where our parameter-free methods are competitive with strong performance in terms of acceleration and universality. Finally, we contribute a sharper guarantee for the model ensemble, a final step of the grid search framework, under interpolated variance characterization.

</details>


### [53] [Covariance-Based Structural Equation Modeling in Small-Sample Settings with $p>n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16894)
*Hiroki Hasegawa,Aoba Tamura,Yukihiko Okada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的结构方程建模估计方法，解决小样本（p>n）情况下传统似然估计失效的问题，通过重构协方差结构为自协方差和交叉协方差分量，实现稳定估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于因子的结构方程建模（SEM）依赖基于似然的估计方法，需要非奇异样本协方差矩阵，但在小样本设置（p>n）下会失效。需要开发能够在小样本情况下稳定估计的方法。

Method: 提出新的估计原则：将协方差结构重新表述为自协方差和交叉协方差分量。构建基于似然的可行集，结合相对误差约束，实现在p>n的小样本设置中对符号和方向的稳定估计。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据上的实验显示，该方法提高了估计稳定性，特别是在恢复结构参数的符号和方向方面表现优异。将基于协方差的SEM扩展到小样本设置。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了小样本情况下SEM估计的稳定性问题，为决策制定提供了实用的方向性信息，扩展了协方差基SEM的应用范围。

Abstract: Factor-based Structural Equation Modeling (SEM) relies on likelihood-based estimation assuming a nonsingular sample covariance matrix, which breaks down in small-sample settings with $p>n$. To address this, we propose a novel estimation principle that reformulates the covariance structure into self-covariance and cross-covariance components. The resulting framework defines a likelihood-based feasible set combined with a relative error constraint, enabling stable estimation in small-sample settings where $p>n$ for sign and direction. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show improved stability, particularly in recovering the sign and direction of structural parameters. These results extend covariance-based SEM to small-sample settings and provide practically useful directional information for decision-making.

</details>


### [54] [Noise-Adaptive Diffusion Sampling for Inverse Problems Without Task-Specific Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16919)
*Yingzhi Xia,Setthakorn Tanomkiattikun,Liangli Zhen,Zaiwang Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出噪声空间哈密顿蒙特卡洛（N-HMC）方法，通过将反向扩散视为从初始噪声到干净图像的确定性映射，在初始噪声空间中进行后验采样，避免局部最优和数据流形不可行问题，并扩展为噪声自适应变体（NA-NHMC）处理未知噪声类型和水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的逆问题求解方法存在局限性：优化方法容易陷入局部最优和噪声过拟合；贝叶斯方法在去噪过程中强制测量一致性会导致流形不可行问题。需要一种能全面探索解空间、保持数据流形可行性的后验采样方法。

Method: 提出N-HMC方法，将反向扩散过程视为从初始噪声到干净图像的确定性映射，在初始噪声空间中进行哈密顿蒙特卡洛采样，使提案保持在学习的数据流形上。进一步扩展为NA-NHMC，通过噪声自适应机制处理未知噪声类型和水平。

Result: 在四个线性和三个非线性逆问题上进行广泛实验，NA-NHMC实现了优越的重建质量，在不同超参数和初始化下表现出鲁棒性能，显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: N-HMC/NA-NHMC通过将推理完全转移到初始噪声空间，有效解决了逆问题求解中的局部最优和流形不可行问题，为基于扩散模型的贝叶斯逆问题求解提供了理论分析和实用框架。

Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs) have recently shown remarkable performance on inverse problems (IPs). Optimization-based methods can fast solve IPs using DMs as powerful regularizers, but they are susceptible to local minima and noise overfitting. Although DMs can provide strong priors for Bayesian approaches, enforcing measurement consistency during the denoising process leads to manifold infeasibility issues. We propose Noise-space Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (N-HMC), a posterior sampling method that treats reverse diffusion as a deterministic mapping from initial noise to clean images. N-HMC enables comprehensive exploration of the solution space, avoiding local optima. By moving inference entirely into the initial-noise space, N-HMC keeps proposals on the learned data manifold. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis of our approach and extend the framework to a noise-adaptive variant (NA-NHMC) that effectively handles IPs with unknown noise type and level. Extensive experiments across four linear and three nonlinear inverse problems demonstrate that NA-NHMC achieves superior reconstruction quality with robust performance across different hyperparameters and initializations, significantly outperforming recent state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/NA-HMC/NA-HMC.

</details>


### [55] [D-QRELO: Training- and Data-Free Delta Compression for Large Language Models via Quantization and Residual Low-Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16940)
*Junlin Li,Shuangyong Song,Guodong Du,Ngai Wong,Xuebo Liu,Yongxiang Li,Min Zhang,Jing Li,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DQRELO：一种无需训练和数据的delta压缩方法，通过粗粒度1比特量化捕获delta主导结构，再用残差低秩近似恢复细节，解决大规模SFT数据集导致的delta压缩难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调加速了特定任务大语言模型开发，但大量微调模型带来巨大内存开销。现有delta压缩方法在处理大规模SFT数据集时失效，因为更大规模数据会放大delta参数幅度、奇异值和熵，加剧压缩误差。

Method: 提出DQRELO方法：1）粗粒度1比特量化捕获delta主导结构；2）残差低秩近似补偿恢复细节；3）无需训练和数据；4）适用于密集和MoE架构。

Result: 在各种LLM（密集和MoE架构）跨多个领域的实验中，DQRELO在挑战性设置下优于现有方法。通过实证分析建立了delta压缩的关键设计原则，展示了任务难度、架构和层位置如何创建可预测模式。

Conclusion: DQRELO有效解决了大规模SFT数据集下的delta压缩问题，其设计原则可指导生产系统中的最优压缩策略，为高效存储和部署微调模型提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) accelerates taskspecific large language models (LLMs) development, but the resulting proliferation of finetuned models incurs substantial memory overhead. Delta compression addresses this by retaining a single pre-trained LLM with multiple compressed delta weights. However, existing methods fail on models fine-tuned with largescale datasets. We find that larger SFT data scale amplifies delta parameter magnitude, singular values, and entropy, exacerbating compression errors. To tackle this, we propose DQRELO (Delta Compression via Quantization and Residual Low-Rank), a novel training- and data-free delta compression method. It combines coarse-grained one-bit quantization to capture the dominant structure of the delta, followed by compensated residual low-rank approximation to recover fine-grained details from the smaller residual error. Experiments on various LLMs spanning dense and MoE architectures across multiple domains under this challenging setting demonstrate that DQRELO outperforms existing methods. Moreover, we establish key design principles for delta compression through extensive empirical analysis, demonstrating how task difficulty, architecture, and layer positioning create predictable patterns that can guide optimal compression strategies in production systems.

</details>


### [56] [Open-TQ-Metal: Fused Compressed-Domain Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16957)
*Sai Vegasena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Open-TQ-Metal首次在Apple Silicon上实现融合压缩域注意力，支持单台64GB Mac运行128K上下文Llama 3.1 70B推理，通过int4 KV缓存量化和自定义Metal计算着色器实现48倍注意力加速和3.2倍内存压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理框架无法在单台64GB消费级Mac上支持128K上下文的Llama 3.1 70B推理，需要解决KV缓存内存占用过大和注意力计算效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出Open-TQ-Metal框架，实时将KV缓存量化为int4，通过自定义Metal计算着色器直接在压缩表示上计算注意力，消除所有中间反量化矩阵。采用融合的sdpa_int4内核实现压缩域注意力计算。

Result: 在330个实验中，sdpa_int4内核在128K上下文下实现48倍注意力加速，KV缓存内存从40GB降至12.5GB（3.2倍压缩），保持与FP16推理相同的top-1 token预测。首次跨架构分析揭示注意力缩放因子（而非模型大小）决定角度量化方案的成功与否。

Conclusion: Open-TQ-Metal首次在Apple Silicon上实现高效的大上下文推理，为消费级硬件上的大语言模型部署提供了可行方案，同时揭示了注意力缩放因子对量化方案选择的关键影响。

Abstract: We present Open-TQ-Metal, the first implementation of fused compressed-domain attention on Apple Silicon, enabling 128K-context inference for Llama 3.1 70B on a single 64GB consumer Mac -- a configuration impossible with all existing inference frameworks. Open-TQ-Metal quantizes the KV cache to int4 on the fly and computes attention directly on the compressed representation via custom Metal compute shaders, eliminating all intermediate dequantization matrices. Across 330 experiments spanning two model families (Gemma 4 31B and Llama 3.1 70B), the fused sdpa_int4 kernel achieves 48x attention speedup at 128K context over the dequantize-then-attend baseline, reduces KV cache memory from 40 GB to 12.5 GB (3.2x compression), and maintains identical top-1 token predictions to FP16 inference. We further provide the first cross-architecture analysis of KV cache quantization methods, revealing that the attention scale factor -- not model size -- determines whether angular quantization schemes like PolarQuant succeed or fail, with Gemma 4's attn_scale=1.0 amplifying directional error 25-100x more than Llama's standard 1/sqrt(d) scaling.

</details>


### [57] [Hyperbolic Enhanced Representation Learning for Incomplete Multi-view Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16959)
*Tianyi Chen,Haobo Wang,Kai Tang,Gengyu Lyu,Tianlei Hu,Gang Chen,Hong Ma,Meixiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERL：一种用于不完全多视图聚类的双曲增强表示学习框架，通过在庞加莱球中构建结构感知的潜在空间来解决欧几里得方法的几何不匹配问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不完全多视图聚类方法主要基于欧几里得空间，但现实世界数据具有内在层次结构，导致几何不匹配问题。这种不匹配会造成语义模糊，即表示会漂移到空间上邻近但语义不同的邻居，从而影响聚类性能。

Method: HERL框架在庞加莱球中操作，构建结构感知的潜在空间。它设计了双约束双曲对比机制：1）基于角度的损失通过方向对齐保持语义身份；2）基于距离的损失强制层次紧凑性。此外，引入双曲原型头来对齐跨视图的层次感知原型分布，纠正全局结构漂移。

Result: 广泛的实验结果表明，HERL在多个数据集上持续优于最先进的方法，证明了其在处理不完全多视图聚类任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: HERL通过双曲几何建模解决了不完全多视图聚类中的几何不匹配问题，解耦了细粒度语义相关性以锐化聚类边界，并通过几何约束纠正了数据恢复过程，为层次结构数据的表示学习提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Incomplete Multi-View Clustering (IMVC) faces the challenge of learning discriminative representations from fragmentary observations while maintaining robustness against missing views. However, prevalent Euclidean-based methods suffer from a geometric mismatch when modeling real-world data with intrinsic hierarchies, leading to semantic blurring where representations drift towards spatially proximal but semantically distinct neighbors. To bridge this gap, we propose HERL, a Hyperbolic Enhanced Representation Learning framework for IMVC. Operating within the Poincaré ball, HERL constructs a structure-aware latent space to enhance representation learning. Specifically, we design a dual-constraint hyperbolic contrastive mechanism optimizing: an angular-based loss to preserve semantic identity via directional alignment, and a distance-based loss to enforce hierarchical compactness. Furthermore, a hyperbolic prototype head is introduced to rectify global structural drift by aligning cross-view hierarchy-aware prototype distributions. Consequently, HERL disentangles fine-grained semantic correlations to sharpen cluster boundaries and imposes geometric constraints to rectify the data recovery process. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that HERL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [58] [Evaluating Multimodal LLMs for Inpatient Diagnosis: Real-World Performance, Safety, and Cost Across Ten Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16980)
*Bruce A. Bassett,Amy Rouillard,Sitwala Mundia,Michael Cameron Gramanie,Linda Camara,Ziyaad Dangor,Shabir A. Madhi,Kajal Morar,Marlvin T. Ncube,Ismail Kalla,Haroon Saloojee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在真实世界LMIC住院患者数据中，多模态大语言模型诊断性能相近且优于常规临床诊断，成本效益比和部署约束比微小性能差异更重要


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估多模态大语言模型在真实世界低收入和中等收入国家公立医院环境中的诊断支持能力，现有研究缺乏对此类场景的全面评估

Method: 回顾性研究VALID，使用南非三级公立医院539例多模态住院病例数据，包括影像、报告、实验室结果、临床记录和生命体征。专家小组裁定300例建立金标准，10个多模态LLM零样本生成输出，三模型LLM评审团进行超过10,000次评估

Result: LLM性能紧密聚集（<15%差异）且成本差异大但性能相近；所有LLM在平均诊断和安全评分上显著优于常规病房诊断；GPT-5.1表现最佳，其次为Gemini模型；添加放射学报告提升性能6%；诊断与推理评分高度相关（ρ=0.85）

Conclusion: 在真实世界LMIC数据集中，多模态LLM表现出相似的诊断性能且优于常规护理，在LMIC环境中可负担性、鲁棒性和部署约束可能比边际性能差异更重要

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed for diagnostic support, but few evaluations use real-world multimodal inpatient data, particularly in low and middle-income country (LMIC) public hospitals.
  Methods: We conducted VALID, a retrospective evaluation of 539 multimodal inpatient cases from a tertiary public hospital in South Africa. Inputs included radiology imaging (CT, MRI, CXR) and reports, laboratory results, clinical notes, and vital signs. Expert panels adjudicated 300 cases (balanced and discordant subsets) to establish ground truth diagnoses, differentials, and reasoning. Ten multimodal LLMs generated zero-shot outputs. A calibrated three-model LLM Jury scored all outputs and routine ward diagnoses across diagnostic accuracy, differential quality, reasoning, and patient safety (>10,000 evaluations). Primary outcomes were composite scores ($S_3$, $S_4$) and win rates.
  Results: (i) LLM performance was tightly clustered (<15% variation) despite large cost differences; low-cost models performed comparably to top models. (ii) All LLMs significantly outperformed routine ward diagnoses on average diagnostic and safety scores. (iii) Top performance was achieved by GPT-5.1, followed by Gemini models. (vi) Adding radiology reports improved performance by 6%. (v) Diagnostic and reasoning scores were highly correlated ($ρ= 0.85$). (vi) Output rates varied (65-100%) due to input constraints. Results were robust across subsets and evaluation design.
  Conclusions: Across a real-world LMIC dataset, multimodal LLMs showed similar diagnostic performance despite large cost differences and outperformed routine care on average safety metrics. Affordability, robustness, and deployment constraints may outweigh marginal performance differences in LMIC settings.

</details>


### [59] [In-Context Learning Under Regime Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16988)
*Carson Dudley,Yutong Bi,Xiaofeng Liu,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了Transformer模型在非平稳环境中的上下文变化点检测能力，建立了理论保证，并通过实验验证了其在合成数据和真实世界任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Transformer基础模型在时间序列预测、表格预测和连续控制等任务中越来越依赖上下文学习，理解这些模型在非平稳环境中检测和适应机制变化的能力变得至关重要。数据生成过程在未知时间点发生变化，模型需要能够检测变化、丢弃或降权过时证据，并实时适应新动态。

Method: 将问题形式化为上下文变化点检测问题，从理论上证明存在能够解决该问题的Transformer模型。构建的模型复杂度（层数和参数）取决于关于变化点位置的信息水平（从无知识到确切时间）。在合成线性回归和线性动态系统上进行实验验证，并在传染病预测和金融波动率预测等真实世界任务中测试。

Result: 理论分析表明存在能够解决上下文变化点检测问题的Transformer模型。实验结果显示，训练的Transformer在不同信息水平下都能匹配最优基线的性能。在真实世界任务中，编码和整合变化点知识确实提高了预训练基础模型的性能，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 论文为Transformer模型在非平稳环境中的上下文变化点检测能力提供了理论保证和实证验证，展示了其在处理真实世界机制变化方面的实际适用性，特别是在传染病预测和金融波动率预测等应用中。

Abstract: Non-stationary sequences arise naturally in control, forecasting, and decision-making. The data-generating process shifts at unknown times, and models must detect the change, discard or downweight obsolete evidence, and adapt to new dynamics on the fly. Transformer-based foundation models increasingly rely on in-context learning for time series forecasting, tabular prediction, and continuous control. As these models are deployed in non-stationary environments, understanding their ability to detect and adapt to regime shifts is important. We formalize this as an in-context change-point detection problem and formally establish the existence of transformer models that solve this problem. Our construction demonstrates that model complexity, in layers and parameters, depends on the level of information available about the change-point location, from no knowledge to knowing exact timing. We validate our results with experiments on synthetic linear regression and linear dynamical systems, where trained transformers match the performance of optimal baselines across information levels. We also show that encoding and incorporating changepoint knowledge indeed improves the real-world performance of a pretrained foundation models on infectious disease forecasting and on financial volatility forecasting around Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC) announcements without retraining, demonstrating practical applicability to real-world regime changes.

</details>


### [60] [Live LTL Progress Tracking: Towards Task-Based Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17106)
*Noel Brindise,Cedric Langbort,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Live LTL Progress Tracking框架，用于跟踪强化学习中非马尔可夫目标的进度，通过跟踪向量表示有限线性时序逻辑规范在轨迹中的执行状态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习中非马尔可夫目标的挑战，需要跟踪智能体在复杂多阶段任务中的进度，为性能度量、多样化探索和奖励塑造提供工具

Method: 基于有限线性时序逻辑(LTL)规范，建立"跟踪向量"框架，在轨迹展开的每个时间步更新向量值，表示规范状态(真、假或"开放")，应用于LTL公式树编码任务执行信息

Result: 正式提出了Live LTL Progress Tracking框架和算法，提供了简单工作示例，展示了其集成到RL模型的途径，为任务空间探索和多样化解决方案发现奠定基础

Conclusion: 该框架为强化学习中非马尔可夫目标的进度跟踪提供了系统方法，具有在性能度量、多样化探索和奖励塑造方面的应用潜力，未来将应用于任务空间探索和多样化解决方案发现

Abstract: Motivated by the challenge presented by non-Markovian objectives in reinforcement learning (RL), we present a novel framework to track and represent the progress of autonomous agents through complex, multi-stage tasks. Given a specification in finite linear temporal logic (LTL), the framework establishes a 'tracking vector' which updates at each time step in a trajectory rollout. The values of the vector represent the status of the specification as the trajectory develops, assigning true, false, or 'open' labels (where 'open' is used for indeterminate cases). Applied to an LTL formula tree, the tracking vector can be used to encode detailed information about how a task is executed over a trajectory, providing a potential tool for new performance metrics, diverse exploration, and reward shaping. In this paper, we formally present the framework and algorithm, collectively named Live LTL Progress Tracking, give a simple working example, and demonstrate avenues for its integration into RL models. Future work will apply the framework to problems such as task-space exploration and diverse solution-finding in RL.

</details>


### [61] [The Topological Trouble With Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17121)
*Michael C. Mozer,Shoaib Ahmed Siddiqui,Rosanne Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文主张循环架构优于前馈Transformer，以解决状态跟踪问题，并提出循环架构分类法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的前馈架构在动态状态跟踪方面存在根本限制，无法有效处理序列依赖关系，导致状态信息被推入深层而变得不可访问

Method: 提出循环和连续思维Transformer架构的分类法，按循环轴（深度vs步骤）和输入token与循环步骤的比例进行分类

Result: 建立了循环架构的分类框架，并识别了增强状态空间模型和粗粒度循环等有前景的研究方向

Conclusion: 时间扩展认知需要从显性思维轨迹转向通过循环架构实现的隐式激活动态，以更好地将状态跟踪集成到现代基础模型中

Abstract: Transformers encode structure in sequences via an expanding contextual history. However, their purely feedforward architecture fundamentally limits dynamic state tracking. State tracking -- the iterative updating of latent variables reflecting an evolving environment -- involves inherently sequential dependencies that feedforward networks struggle to maintain. Consequently, feedforward models push evolving state representations deeper into their layer stack with each new input step, rendering information inaccessible in shallow layers and ultimately exhausting the model's depth. While this depth limit can be bypassed by dynamic depth models and by explicit or latent thinking that externalizes state representations, these solutions are computationally and memory inefficient. In this article, we argue that temporally extended cognition requires refocusing from explicit thought traces to implicit activation dynamics via recurrent architectures. We introduce a taxonomy of recurrent and continuous-thought transformer architectures, categorizing them by their recurrence axis (depth versus step) and their ratio of input tokens to recurrence steps. Finally, we outline promising research directions, including enhanced state-space models and coarse-grained recurrence, to better integrate state tracking into modern foundation models.

</details>


### [62] [BOIL: Learning Environment Personalized Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17137)
*Rohan Patil,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BOIL方法，通过PageRank算法和公共信息最大化从环境结构中提取有价值信息，指导多智能体系统在复杂环境中的长期行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在复杂环境中导航面临挑战，需要从有限信息中高效提取洞察。现有方法在处理覆盖、巡逻和随机可达性等问题时，难以有效指导长期行为

Method: 提出Blackbox Oracle Information Learning (BOIL)过程，利用PageRank算法和公共信息最大化，从环境结构中提取有价值信息，生成指导智能体长期行为的策略分布

Result: 实验证明BOIL在生成促进长期性能改进的策略分布方面有效，在复杂环境中超越启发式方法

Conclusion: BOIL为多智能体系统在复杂环境中导航提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，能够从环境结构中提取有价值信息来指导长期行为

Abstract: Navigating complex environments poses challenges for multi-agent systems, requiring efficient extraction of insights from limited information. In this paper, we introduce the Blackbox Oracle Information Learning (BOIL) process, a scalable solution for extracting valuable insights from the environment structure. Leveraging the Pagerank algorithm and common information maximization, BOIL facilitates the extraction of information to guide long-term agent behavior applicable to problems such as coverage, patrolling, and stochastic reachability. Through experiments, we demonstrate the efficacy of BOIL in generating strategy distributions conducive to improved performance over extended time horizons, surpassing heuristic approaches in complex environments.

</details>


### [63] [RosettaSearch: Multi-Objective Inference-Time Search for Protein Sequence Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17175)
*Meghana Kshirsagar,Allen Nie,Ching-An Cheng,Fanglei Xue,Rahul Dodhia,Juan Lavista Ferres,Kevin K. Yang,Frank DiMaio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RosettaSearch是一种用于蛋白质序列优化的推理时多目标优化方法，利用大型语言模型作为生成优化器，结合RosettaFold3结构预测模型进行奖励计算，显著提升蛋白质设计成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质序列设计方法（如LigandMPNN）的单次解码可能产生次优序列，需要一种能够进行受控探索和利用的优化方法来恢复高质量设计，提高结构保真度和设计成功率。

Method: 使用大型语言模型作为生成优化器，结合搜索算法进行受控探索和利用，利用RosettaFold3结构预测模型计算奖励，通过多模态扩展将预测蛋白质结构图像作为反馈指导序列生成。

Result: 在400个LigandMPNN生成的次优序列上，RosettaSearch恢复出高质量设计，结构保真度指标提升18%-68%，设计成功率提高2.5倍；结果在不同LLM家族（o4-mini和Gemini-3）和独立结构预测器（Chai-1）上保持稳健，并推广到计算生成的蛋白质骨架。

Conclusion: RosettaSearch是一种有效的蛋白质序列优化方法，能够显著提高设计成功率，推广到不同模型和蛋白质骨架类型，其生成的序列轨迹可作为训练数据，为蛋白质设计领域提供有价值的工具。

Abstract: We introduce RosettaSearch, an inference-time multi-objective optimization approach for protein sequence optimization. We use large language models (LLMs) as a generative optimizer within a search algorithm capable of controlled exploration and exploitation, using rewards computed from RosettaFold3, a structure prediction model. In a large-scale evaluation, we apply RosettaSearch to 400 suboptimal sequences generated by LigandMPNN (a state-of-the-art model trained for protein sequence design), recovering high-fidelity designs that LigandMPNN's single-pass decoding fails to produce. RosettaSearch's designs show improvements in structural fidelity metrics ranging between 18\% to 68\%, translating to a 2.5$\times$ improvement in design success rate. We observe that these gains in success rate are robust when RosettaSearch-designed sequences are evaluated with an independent structure prediction oracle (Chai-1) and generalize across two distinct LLM families (o4-mini and Gemini-3), with performance scaling consistently with reasoning capability. We further demonstrate that RosettaSearch improves sequence fidelity for ProteinMPNN-designed sequences on \textit{de novo} backbones from the Dayhoff atlas, showing that the approach generalizes beyond native protein structures to computationally generated backbones. We also demonstrate a multi-modal extension of RosettaSearch with vision-language models, where images of predicted protein structures are used as feedback to incorporate structural context to guide protein sequence generation. The sequence trajectories generated by our approach can be used as training data in sequence design models or in post-training and will be released along with the code and datasets upon publication.

</details>


### [64] [Decomposing the Depth Profile of Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17177)
*Jayadev Billa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现微调过程中表征变化主要集中输出层附近，这种模式在大多数标准训练中普遍存在，其形态受架构类型、模型规模和目标函数影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究微调过程中表征变化的深度分布模式：这种模式是模型的内在属性还是梯度流动幅度的结果？需要直接测试并量化这种变化在不同架构、规模和目标下的表现

Method: 通过240次微调实验，涵盖15个模型、4种架构家族（编码器和解码器Transformer、状态空间模型、RNN），参数规模从125M到6.9B。应用每层控制使各层ΔW/W在优化器步骤后相等，分析标准训练和控制条件下的变化模式

Result: 1. 标准训练中，除一次例外，表征变化都集中在输出层附近；2. 在控制条件下，125M-350M规模的序列块架构（BERT、OPT、GPT-2）在所有测试目标下保持斜率，而并行块架构（Pythia、CodeGen）仅在因果语言建模目标下保持；3. 1.3B-1.4B规模时，两种块类型在CausalLM下都显示正斜率；4. 标准训练中，剖面形状由两个额外轴描述：陡峭度跟踪初始化时的训练无关目标距离，剖面宽度主要由架构决定

Conclusion: 表征变化的局部性梯度（深度方向斜率）是一个复合现象，其组成部分具有尺度依赖性。微调过程中的表征变化模式既受模型内在属性影响，也受梯度流动影响，具体表现取决于架构类型、模型规模和目标函数

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts pretrained networks to new objectives. Whether the resulting depth profile of representational change reflects an intrinsic property of the model or the magnitude of gradient flow has not been tested directly. We measure this profile across 240 fine-tuning runs spanning 15 models in four architecture families (encoder and decoder transformers, a state-space model, and an RNN) at scales from 125M to 6.9B parameters. Representational change concentrates in output-proximal layers in every standard-training run except one. We apply a per-layer control that equalizes $\|ΔW\|/\|W\|$ across layers after each optimizer step. Under this control, the profile persists in some conditions and collapses in others. At 125M--350M, sequential-block architectures (BERT, OPT, GPT-2) retain the slope across tested objectives while parallel-block architectures (Pythia, CodeGen) retain it only for causal-language-modeling objectives. This architectural distinction narrows at 1.3B--1.4B, where both block types show positive equal-step slopes for CausalLM. Under standard training, profile shape is described by two additional axes: steepness tracks a training-free objective distance at initialization, and profile width is dominated by architecture. We treat the locality gradient, the depthwise slope of representational change, as a composite phenomenon whose components are scale-dependent.

</details>


### [65] [Demystifying the unreasonable effectiveness of online alignment methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17207)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 贪婪迭代对齐方法在实践中效果显著，但现有理论保证的O(log T) KL正则化遗憾相对保守。本文认为这种不匹配源于遗憾准则本身：KL正则化遗憾将学习统计成本与软化训练策略诱导的探索随机化混为一谈。通过研究温度为零的传统遗憾准则，证明标准贪婪在线对齐方法（如在线RLHF和在线DPO）可实现常数(O(1))累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论对贪婪迭代对齐方法的性能保证（O(log T) KL正则化遗憾）过于悲观，与实际观察到的卓越效率不匹配。作者认为这种理论-实践差距源于KL正则化遗憾准则本身的问题：它混淆了学习统计成本和训练策略软化引入的探索随机化效应。需要更合适的性能度量来分离这些效应，从而更准确地解释贪婪对齐方法的实际效率。

Method: 采用温度为零的传统遗憾准则，该准则仅评估推理时排名最高的响应，从而分离学习统计成本与正则化诱导的随机化。在此决策中心性能度量下，分析标准贪婪在线对齐方法（包括在线RLHF和在线DPO）的理论性能。通过数学证明展示这些方法在传统遗憾准则下的常数累积遗憾。

Result: 理论证明表明，在温度为零的传统遗憾准则下，标准贪婪在线对齐方法（在线RLHF和在线DPO）能够实现常数(O(1))累积遗憾。这一结果比现有的O(log T) KL正则化遗憾保证更为乐观，更准确地反映了这些方法在实际应用中的高效性。

Conclusion: 通过分离学习统计成本与正则化诱导的随机化效应，本文为贪婪对齐方法的卓越实践效率提供了更精确的理论解释。温度为零的传统遗憾准则能够更好地评估决策质量，在此准则下贪婪在线对齐方法具有常数遗憾，这解释了为什么它们在实践中比现有理论预测更为高效。

Abstract: Iterative alignment methods based on purely greedy updates are remarkably effective in practice, yet existing theoretical guarantees of \(O(\log T)\) KL-regularized regret can seem pessimistic relative to their empirical performance. In this paper, we argue that this mismatch arises from the regret criterion itself: KL-regularized regret conflates the statistical cost of learning with the exploratory randomization induced by the softened training policy. To separate these effects, we study the traditional temperature-zero regret criterion, which evaluates only the top-ranked response at inference time. Under this decision-centric notion of performance, we prove that standard greedy online alignment methods, including online RLHF and online DPO, achieve constant \((O(1))\) cumulative regret. By isolating the cost of identifying the best response from the stochasticity induced by regularization, our results provide a sharper theoretical explanation for the practical superb efficiency of greedy alignment.

</details>


### [66] [Guardrails in Logit Space: Safety Token Regularization for LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17210)
*Thong Bach,Truyen Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出安全令牌正则化(STR)方法，在微调LLMs时通过约束拒绝模板中的关键令牌来保持模型的安全对齐性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，即使在良性数据集上微调对齐良好的大语言模型，也会降低其安全对齐性。现有的安全对齐技术主要关注预训练阶段，而微调后的模型容易受到行为偏移的影响，需要一种在微调过程中保持安全属性的轻量级方法。

Method: 提出安全令牌正则化(STR)方法：1) 从对齐良好模型的拒绝模板中识别关键令牌；2) 在训练过程中约束这些令牌相关的logits，防止关键安全行为的丢失。该方法计算开销小，可与LoRA等参数高效微调技术无缝集成。

Result: 综合实验表明：1) STR的安全性能与最先进方法相当；2) 保持任务特定效用；3) 实现开销最小；4) 增强训练稳定性和整体性能；5) 超越单纯的安全考虑。

Conclusion: 安全令牌正则化为微调LLMs提供了一种实用且易于部署的持续安全对齐策略，解决了微调过程中安全对齐性下降的问题，同时保持了模型效用和训练稳定性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning well-aligned large language models (LLMs) on new domains often degrades their safety alignment, even when using benign datasets. Existing safety alignment techniques primarily focus on pretraining, leaving fine-tuned models vulnerable to behavioral shifts. In this work, we introduce safety token regularization (STR), a lightweight method designed to preserve safety properties during fine-tuning. Our approach identifies salient tokens from rejection templates of well-aligned models and constrains their associated logits during training, preventing the loss of critical safety behaviors. Unlike reinforcement learning or preference optimization methods, STR requires minimal additional computation and seamlessly integrates with parameter-efficient fine-tuning techniques such as LoRA. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves safety performance on par with state-of-the-art methods, while preserving task-specific utility and requiring minimal implementation overhead. Furthermore, we show that safety token regularization enhances training stability and overall performance beyond safety considerations alone. This work offers a practical and readily deployable strategy for continual safety alignment in fine-tuned LLMs.

</details>


### [67] [Continual Safety Alignment via Gradient-Based Sample Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17215)
*Thong Bach,Dung Nguyen,Thao Minh Le,Truyen Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于梯度的样本选择方法，在微调时过滤高梯度样本，有效保持大语言模型的安全对齐性同时维持任务性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要持续适应新任务同时保持安全对齐，但微调即使是良性数据也常损害安全行为（拒绝有害请求、真实性、常识推理）。需要研究哪些训练样本导致对齐漂移

Method: 通过数据中心视角分析样本对安全对齐的影响，发现高梯度样本导致更大安全退化并驱动模型向预训练分布回归，而中等梯度样本能实现任务学习且对齐损失最小。提出基于梯度的样本选择方法，在微调过程中过滤高梯度样本

Result: 在多个模型家族的持续领域任务上，该方法显著改善对齐保持，同时保持竞争力的任务性能，无需策划安全数据或架构修改。方法在选择比例、任务顺序和多样化攻击基准上均表现稳健

Conclusion: 基于梯度的样本选择是保持大语言模型安全对齐的有效方法，通过识别和过滤导致对齐漂移的高梯度样本，实现任务适应与安全性的平衡

Abstract: Large language models require continuous adaptation to new tasks while preserving safety alignment. However, fine-tuning on even benign data often compromises safety behaviors, including refusal of harmful requests, truthfulness, and commonsense reasoning. We investigate which training samples cause alignment drift through a data-centric lens. Our empirical analysis shows samples contribute unequally: high-gradient samples cause greater safety degradation and drive models toward pretrained distributions, while moderate-gradient samples enable task learning with minimal alignment loss. We propose gradient-based sample selection that filters high-gradient samples during fine-tuning. Across multiple model families on continual domain tasks, our method substantially improves alignment preservation while maintaining competitive task performance, without requiring curated safe data or architectural modifications. Our method is robust across selection ratios, task orderings, and diverse attack benchmarks.

</details>


### [68] [LASER: Low-Rank Activation SVD for Efficient Recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17224)
*Ege Çakar,Ketan Ali Raghu,Lia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRMs的递归展开过程中，激活占据有效线性低维子空间，可通过廉价幂迭代动态跟踪主方向。作者提出LASER框架，利用矩阵自由子空间跟踪和保真度触发重置机制，实现约60%激活内存节省且无显著精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Tiny Recursive Models (TRMs)等递归架构在隐式推理过程中激活流形的几何结构，理解权重共享如何影响迭代计算在特征方向上的集中，并探索利用这种结构提高计算效率和稳定性的可能性。

Method: 提出LASER（低秩激活SVD高效递归）动态压缩框架：1）通过廉价幂迭代动态跟踪激活子空间的主方向；2）使用矩阵自由子空间跟踪维护演化中的低秩基；3）采用保真度触发重置机制确保压缩质量。

Result: 发现TRMs激活占据有效线性低维子空间，权重共享使迭代计算集中在少量主导特征方向上，且这种集中程度在不同计算位置差异显著。LASER框架实现约60%激活内存节省，无统计显著精度下降。

Conclusion: 递归架构在隐式推理过程中表现出高度结构化的激活模式，这种几何结构可被有效利用进行动态压缩。研究提出了关于递归架构如何分配表示容量以及如何利用这种集中性提高隐式计算效率和稳定性的新问题。

Abstract: Recursive architectures such as Tiny Recursive Models (TRMs) perform implicit reasoning through iterative latent computation, yet the geometric structure of these reasoning trajectories remains poorly understood. We investigate the activation manifold of TRMs during recursive unrolling and find that activations occupy an effectively linear, low-dimensional subspace whose principal directions can be tracked dynamically with cheap power iterations. This suggests that weight-sharing concentrates iterative computation along a small number of dominant eigendirections, and we find that this concentration varies sharply across computational sites. We exploit this structure through LASER (Low-Rank Activation SVD for Efficient Recursion), a dynamic compression framework that maintains an evolving low-rank basis via matrix-free subspace tracking with a fidelity-triggered reset mechanism, achieving ${\sim}60\%$ activation memory savings with no statistically significant accuracy degradation. Our analysis raises questions about how recursive architectures allocate representational capacity during implicit reasoning, and whether this concentration can be exploited to improve the efficiency and stability of latent computation.

</details>


### [69] [REALM: Reliable Expertise-Aware Language Model Fine-Tuning from Noisy Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17289)
*Sajjad Ghiasvand,Mark Beliaev,Mahnoosh Alizadeh,Ramtin Pedarsani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REALM方法通过无监督学习标注者专业度，在噪声标注下提升语言模型微调效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标注流程涉及不同专业水平的众包工作者，标准实践通过多数投票或简单平均聚合标签，丢弃了标注者身份信息，导致模型直接吸收不可靠标注者的错误

Method: 提出REALM方法，无监督联合学习模型参数和每个标注者的标量专业度值；将每个观察到的标签建模为模型预测和均匀随机猜测的混合，权重由标注者学习到的专业度决定；扩展到多任务设置，通过学习的专业度矩阵捕捉每个标注者在不同任务上的可靠性

Result: 在五个问答基准测试中，使用三种规模的Flan-T5在模拟噪声标注下进行微调；REALM在大多数单任务和多任务设置中持续优于朴素的噪声SFT，在最具对抗性的情况下准确率提升高达50%，且增益随模型容量增长

Conclusion: REALM通过无监督学习标注者专业度，有效减轻噪声标注对语言模型微调的负面影响，特别是在多任务和对抗性标注场景下表现优异

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of large language models relies on human-annotated data, yet annotation pipelines routinely involve multiple crowdworkers of heterogeneous expertise. Standard practice aggregates labels via majority vote or simple averaging, discarding annotator identity and causing the model to absorb the errors of unreliable annotators directly into its parameters. We propose REALM, a method that jointly learns the model parameters and a scalar expertise value for each annotator entirely unsupervised, requiring no supervision beyond annotator identity. The key idea is to model each observed label as a mixture between the model's prediction and a uniform random guess, weighted by the annotator's learned expertise. We extend REALM to a multi-task setting via a learned expertise matrix that captures per-annotator reliability across tasks. We evaluate on five question answering benchmarks, fine-tuning three sizes of Flan-T5 under simulated noisy annotations. The proposed algorithm consistently outperforms the naive noisy SFT in the large majority of single- and multi-task settings, across datasets, model sizes, and noise types, with accuracy improvements of up to $50\%$ in the most adversarial regime and gains that grow with model capacity.

</details>


### [70] [Interpolating Discrete Diffusion Models with Controllable Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17310)
*Marcel Kollovieh,Sirine Ayadi,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IDDM是一种插值离散扩散模型，通过可控重采样机制减少对中间潜在状态的依赖，在分子图生成和文本生成任务中表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散模型存在根本性限制：掩码扩散模型因早期解掩码导致不可逆错误，均匀扩散模型虽能自校正但过度依赖中间潜在状态导致样本质量低

Method: 提出IDDM（插值离散扩散模型），核心是可控重采样机制，将部分概率质量重置到边缘分布，减少误差累积并实现更有效的标记校正。生成过程在保持当前状态、从先验重采样和向目标状态翻转之间插值，同时保持边缘一致性并完全解耦训练与推理

Result: 在分子图生成和文本生成任务上，IDDM与最先进的离散扩散模型相比表现出竞争力

Conclusion: IDDM通过减少对中间潜在状态的依赖，改进了离散扩散模型，解决了现有方法的局限性，在多个领域展现出优异性能

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models form a powerful class of generative models across diverse domains, including text and graphs. However, existing approaches face fundamental limitations. Masked diffusion models suffer from irreversible errors due to early unmasking, while uniform diffusion models, despite enabling self-correction, often yield low-quality samples due to their strong reliance on intermediate latent states. We introduce IDDM, an Interpolating Discrete Diffusion Model, that improves diffusion by reducing dependence on intermediate latent states. Central to IDDM is a controllable resampling mechanism that partially resets probability mass to the marginal distribution, mitigating error accumulation and enabling more effective token corrections. IDDM specifies a generative process whose transitions interpolate between staying at the current state, resampling from a prior, and flipping toward the target state, while enforcing marginal consistency and fully decoupling training from inference. We benchmark our model against state-of-the-art discrete diffusion models across molecular graph generation as well as text generation tasks, demonstrating competitive performance.

</details>


### [71] [A Survey of Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Models under Data Scarcity: Challenges and Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17312)
*Zhiyin Yu,Yuchen Mou,Juncheng Yan,Junyu Luo,Chunchun Chen,Xing Wei,Yunhui Liu,Hongru Sun,Yuxing Zhang,Jun Xu,Yatao Bian,Ming Zhang,Wei Ye,Tieke He,Jie Yang,Guanjie Zheng,Zhonghai Wu,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 关于大型语言模型在数据稀缺条件下的强化学习方法的首次系统性综述，提出了一个三层框架来分类和分析现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为增强大型语言模型推理能力的重要后训练范式，但面临严重的数据稀缺挑战，包括高质量外部监督的有限性和模型生成经验的约束性，这使得数据高效的强化学习成为关键研究方向。

Method: 提出了一个自下而上的分层框架，围绕三个互补视角构建：数据中心视角、训练中心视角和框架中心视角。开发了现有方法的分类体系，总结了每个类别的代表性方法，并分析了它们的优势和局限性。

Result: 建立了数据高效强化学习LLMs的设计空间概念基础，为这一新兴领域的研究人员提供了清晰的指导框架，并提出了全面的未来研究方向路线图。

Conclusion: 该综述为数据稀缺条件下LLMs的强化学习提供了系统性的分类和分析框架，旨在促进更高效、可扩展的强化学习后训练方法的发展，为未来研究提供指导和灵感。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful post-training paradigm for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, reinforcement learning for LLMs faces substantial data scarcity challenges, including the limited availability of high-quality external supervision and the constrained volume of model-generated experience. These limitations make data-efficient reinforcement learning a critical research direction. In this survey, we present the first systematic review of reinforcement learning for LLMs under data scarcity. We propose a bottom-up hierarchical framework built around three complementary perspectives: the data-centric perspective, the training-centric perspective, and the framework-centric perspective. We develop a taxonomy of existing methods, summarize representative approaches in each category, and analyze their strengths and limitations. Our taxonomy aims to provide a clear conceptual foundation for understanding the design space of data-efficient RL for LLMs and to guide researchers working in this emerging area. We hope this survey offers a comprehensive roadmap for future research and inspires new directions toward more efficient and scalable reinforcement learning post-training for LLMs.

</details>


### [72] [SigGate-GT: Taming Over-Smoothing in Graph Transformers via Sigmoid-Gated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17324)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SigGate-GT：一种在图Transformer中引入逐头sigmoid门控机制的新方法，通过抑制无信息连接来解决过平滑和注意力熵退化问题，在多个基准测试中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图Transformer在分子和长距离推理任务上表现优异，但受到过平滑（节点表示随深度增加而逐渐坍缩）和注意力熵退化的困扰。研究发现这些问题与大型语言模型中的注意力汇（attention sinks）有共同根源：softmax注意力的和为1约束迫使每个节点都必须关注某些地方，即使不存在信息性信号。

Method: 提出SigGate-GT，在GraphGPS框架内应用学习的逐头sigmoid门控到注意力输出。每个门可以将激活抑制到零，使注意力头能够选择性地静默无信息连接。该方法借鉴了最近在大型语言模型中使用逐元素sigmoid门控消除注意力汇的发现。

Result: 在五个标准基准测试中：1）在ZINC上达到0.059 MAE，与先前最佳结果持平；2）在ogbg-molhiv上达到82.47% ROC-AUC，创下新的SOTA；3）在所有五个数据集上相对于GraphGPS都有统计显著提升（p<0.05）。消融实验显示：门控将过平滑减少30%（4-16层的平均相对MAD增益），增加注意力熵，并在10倍学习率范围内稳定训练，在OGB上仅增加约1%的参数开销。

Conclusion: 逐头sigmoid门控是解决图Transformer中过平滑和注意力熵退化问题的有效方法。该方法通过允许注意力头选择性地抑制无信息连接，提高了模型性能、稳定性和表示质量，同时保持了参数效率。

Abstract: Graph transformers achieve strong results on molecular and long-range reasoning tasks, yet remain hampered by over-smoothing (the progressive collapse of node representations with depth) and attention entropy degeneration. We observe that these pathologies share a root cause with attention sinks in large language models: softmax attention's sum-to-one constraint forces every node to attend somewhere, even when no informative signal exists. Motivated by recent findings that element-wise sigmoid gating eliminates attention sinks in large language models, we propose SigGate-GT, a graph transformer that applies learned, per-head sigmoid gates to the attention output within the GraphGPS framework. Each gate can suppress activations toward zero, enabling heads to selectively silence uninformative connections. On five standard benchmarks, SigGate-GT matches the prior best on ZINC (0.059 MAE) and sets new state-of-the-art on ogbg-molhiv (82.47% ROC-AUC), with statistically significant gains over GraphGPS across all five datasets ($p < 0.05$). Ablations show that gating reduces over-smoothing by 30% (mean relative MAD gain across 4-16 layers), increases attention entropy, and stabilizes training across a $10\times$ learning rate range, with about 1% parameter overhead on OGB.

</details>


### [73] [Rethinking the Comparison Unit in Sequence-Level Reinforcement Learning: An Equal-Length Paired Training Framework from Loss Correction to Sample Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17328)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang,Sibo wang,Linglin Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了序列级相对强化学习中的长度问题，提出将长度问题视为比较单元构建问题而非损失缩放或归一化偏差，并提出了EqLen方法来解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽然部分缓解了长度相关现象，但未能充分解决一个更根本的问题：训练中使用的比较单元缺乏内在可比性。长度问题不应仅被视为损失缩放或归一化偏差，而应视为比较单元构建问题。

Method: 提出了基于样本构建的训练框架，主动构建等长、可对齐、可比较的训练片段而非对不等长响应进行后处理修正。具体提出EqLen方法，通过双轨同步生成、前缀继承和片段掩码等技术，高效收集有效的等长训练片段并实现稳定训练。

Result: EqLen方法适用于GRPO、GSPO、RLOO等组相对比较算法，能够有效解决序列级相对强化学习中的长度问题。

Conclusion: 长度问题的本质是比较单元构建问题，通过主动构建等长可比较的训练片段而非被动修正不等长响应，可以更有效地解决序列级相对强化学习中的长度偏差问题。

Abstract: This paper investigates the length problem in sequence-level relative reinforcement learning. We observe that, although existing methods partially alleviate length-related phenomena, a more fundamental issue remains insufficiently characterized: the comparison units used during training lack inherent comparability. Building on this observation, we propose a new perspective: the length problem should not be viewed merely as a loss-scaling or normalization bias, but rather as a \emph{comparison unit construction} problem. We further establish a sample-construction-based training framework that, instead of applying post-hoc corrections to unequal-length responses, proactively constructs equal-length, alignable, and comparable training segments during generation. Within this framework, we propose EqLen, a concrete method applicable to group-relative comparison algorithms such as GRPO, GSPO, and RLOO. Through dual-track synchronous generation, prefix inheritance, and segment masking, EqLen efficiently collects effective equal-length training segments and enables stable

</details>


### [74] [FLARE: Task-agnostic embedding model evaluation through a normalization process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17344)
*Jingzhou Jiang,Yixuan Tang,Yi Yang,Kar Yan Tam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FLARE方法，一种无需标签的嵌入模型评估框架，通过归一化流直接估计信息充分性，避免高维空间中的密度估计问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏任务特定标签的情况下，难以为目标语料库选择合适的嵌入模型。现有的无标签评估方法（基于核估计或高斯混合）在高维空间中失效，导致排名不稳定

Method: 提出基于流的无标签表示嵌入评估（FLARE），利用归一化流直接从对数似然估计信息充分性，避免基于距离的密度估计。该方法给出了有限样本边界，表明估计误差取决于数据流形的内在维度而非原始嵌入维度

Result: 在11个数据集和8个嵌入器上，FLARE在监督基准下达到了Spearman相关系数ρ=0.90，并在高维嵌入（d≥3,584）中保持稳定，而现有的无标签基线方法失效

Conclusion: FLARE提供了一种有效的无标签嵌入模型评估方法，能够处理高维空间中的评估问题，其性能依赖于数据的内在维度而非嵌入维度，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: When task-specific labels are not available, it becomes difficult to select an embedding model for a specific target corpus. Existing labelless measures based on kernel estimators or Gaussian mixes fail in high-dimensional space, resulting in unstable rankings. We propose a flow-based labelless representation embedding evaluation (FLARE), which utilizes normalized streams to estimate information sufficiency directly from log-likelihood and avoid distance-based density estimation. We give a finite sample boundary, indicating that the estimation error depends on the intrinsic dimension of the data manifold rather than the original embedding dimension. On 11 datasets and 8 embedders, FLARE reached Spearman's $ρ$ of 0.90 under the supervised benchmark and remained stable in high-dimensional embeddings ($d \geq 3{,}584$) as the existing labelless baseline collapsed.

</details>


### [75] [Towards a Data-Parameter Correspondence for LLMs: A Preliminary Discussion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17384)
*Ou Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了数据-参数对应关系框架，将LLM优化中的数据中心化和模型中心化方法统一为统计流形上的几何对偶操作，揭示了数据修剪与参数稀疏化、上下文学习与LoRA适配、对抗攻击与隐私保护之间的数学等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大语言模型优化分为孤立的数据中心化和模型中心化范式：前者通过选择、增强或投毒操作数据样本，后者通过掩码、量化或低秩适配调整模型权重。这种分离限制了跨领域方法论的转移和协同优化潜力。论文旨在建立统一框架，揭示这些看似不同的操作在统计流形几何结构中的内在联系。

Method: 基于统计流形理论，建立数据-参数对应关系框架：1) 使用Fisher-Rao度量g_ij(θ)和自然参数θ与期望参数η之间的Legendre对偶；2) 识别三种基本对应关系：几何对应（数据修剪与参数稀疏化等价于流形体积缩减）、低秩对应（上下文学习与LoRA适配探索Grassmann流形G(r,d)的相同子空间）、安全隐私对应（对抗攻击与保护机制的协同/衰减关系）；3) 从训练到推理的全生命周期扩展。

Result: 该框架为跨社区方法论转移提供了数学形式化，证明：1) 数据修剪和参数稀疏化通过对偶坐标约束等价地减少流形体积；2) k-shot样本在几何上等价于rank-r更新；3) 对抗攻击在数据投毒和参数后门间表现出协同放大，而保护机制遵循级联衰减，数据压缩乘法增强参数隐私；4) 数据与参数模态的协同优化在效率、鲁棒性和隐私维度上优于孤立方法。

Conclusion: 数据-参数对应关系框架统一了LLM优化的两个传统范式，揭示了它们在统计流形几何结构中的对偶本质。该数学框架不仅解释了现有方法的深层联系，还为跨训练、后训练压缩和推理阶段的协同优化提供了理论基础，有望推动更高效、鲁棒和隐私保护的模型优化方法发展。

Abstract: Large language model optimization has historically bifurcated into isolated data-centric and model-centric paradigms: the former manipulates involved samples through selection, augmentation, or poisoning, while the latter tunes model weights via masking, quantization, or low-rank adaptation. This paper establishes a unified \emph{data-parameter correspondence} revealing these seemingly disparate operations as dual manifestations of the same geometric structure on the statistical manifold $\mathcal{M}$.
