*Xiaoyi Zhang,Amna Irshad,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究使用确定性射线追踪信道模型评估可移动天线系统在现实传播条件下的性能，发现虽然简化信道模型高估了阵列配置间的性能差异，但可移动天线系统在不同用户分布、阵列尺寸和多径条件下仍显著优于传统固定阵列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足未来无线系统日益增长的数据流量需求，需要能够适应复杂传播环境的新型传输架构。可移动天线系统作为一种有前景的方法，通过物理重定位天线元件来利用空间自由度。然而，现有研究大多依赖理想化或简化的信道模型，未能回答可移动天线系统的性能增益在现实传播条件下是否依然存在。

Method: 开发了一个结合三维射线追踪和场响应信道建模的仿真框架，用于研究下行多用户MIMO系统中可移动天线的性能。采用粒子群优化和遗传算法来优化天线位置，使用确定性射线追踪信道模型替代简化的距离基信道模型。

Result: 仿真结果表明：简化的距离基信道模型预测竞争阵列配置之间存在较大的性能差异，但现实的射线追踪信道显著压缩了这些差异，表明传播效应主导了纯阵列几何优化。尽管如此，即使在几何受限的传播环境中，可移动天线系统在不同用户分布、阵列尺寸和多径条件下仍保持对传统固定阵列的显著优势。

Conclusion: 可移动天线系统在现实传播条件下仍然有效，传播效应比纯阵列几何优化更为重要。研究强调了在评估可移动天线系统性能时使用现实信道模型的重要性，并证实了可移动天线系统在复杂无线环境中的实际价值。

Abstract: To meet the growing data traffic demand in future wireless systems, novel transmission architectures capable of adapting to complex propagation environments are required. Movable antenna (MA) systems have recently emerged as a promising approach, enabling the physical repositioning of antenna elements to exploit spatial degrees of freedom. However, existing studies largely rely on idealized or simplistic channel models, leaving open the question of whether the performance gains of MA systems persist under realistic propagation conditions. This paper investigates the performance of downlink multi-user MIMO systems with movable antennas using deterministic ray-traced channel models. A simulation framework combining three-dimensional ray tracing and field-response channel modeling is developed, and antenna positions are optimized using particle swarm optimization and genetic algorithms. Simulation results reveal that while simplified distance-based channel models predict large performance disparities between competing array configurations, realistic ray-traced channels significantly compress these differences, indicating that propagation effects dominate over pure array geometry optimization. Nevertheless, movable antenna systems retain strong effectiveness over conventional fixed arrays across different user distributions, array sizes, and multipath conditions, even in geometry-constrained propagation environments.

</details>


### [151] [CADRE: Card-Agnostic Domain-Aligned RF Embeddings for Virtual PIN Pads on Passive NFC Cards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17192)
*Dickson Akuoko Sarpong,Hongzhi Guo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种用于被动NFC卡的安全密码输入框架，通过虚拟PIN键盘和深度学习识别按压产生的RF信号变化，实现跨卡稳定认证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动NFC卡缺乏主动安全机制，任何持有者都可能被误认为授权用户，需要增强身份验证安全性

Method: 使用虚拟PIN键盘让用户在卡上按压输入密码，通过RF信号阻抗变化检测按压；采用深度学习模型从信号模式推断密码；引入对抗域对齐模块处理不同卡之间的响应差异；开发SDR测试床收集数据；使用基于校准协方差模型的马氏距离进行识别

Result: 系统达到98.20%的识别接受率，在显著噪声退化下保持鲁棒性，完全卡无关且可无缝集成到现有NFC基础设施

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功解决了被动NFC卡的安全限制，通过虚拟PIN键盘和深度学习实现了可靠的身份验证，具有实际部署潜力

Abstract: Near Field Communication (NFC) cards are widely used for identification, but their passive nature often limits the ability to incorporate additional security mechanisms. As a result, anyone holding the card may be incorrectly recognized as an authenticated user. To overcome this limitation, this paper presents a secure manual password input framework using a virtual PIN pad for passive NFC cards. Users input passwords by pressing designated regions on the card, which induces measurable impedance variations in the NFC antenna. These variations change the RF signals subtly, and a deep learning model is used to infer the intended password from the resulting signal patterns. A key challenge is that identical press interactions can produce significantly different responses across NFC cards, which yields unreliable recognition. To address this, we introduce a lightweight recognition approach that operates directly within the RF feature space at the penultimate layer of a temporal neural encoder. An adversarial domain-alignment module reshapes virtual PIN pad press-response embeddings into compact, card-invariant clusters, which enables stable and consistent recognition across heterogeneous cards. To support model training and evaluation, a reconfigurable software-defined radio (SDR) testbed is developed, and PIN pad press-response data are collected from commercially available ISO/IEC 15693 cards. Recognition is performed using a Mahalanobis distance metric derived from a calibration-based covariance model that captures feature correlations. Experimental results show that the proposed system achieves a 98.20\% recognition acceptance rate and remains robust under substantial noise degradation. The framework is fully card-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into existing NFC infrastructures.

</details>


### [152] [SPaRSe-TIME: Saliency-Projected Low-Rank Temporal Modeling for Efficient and Interpretable Time Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17350)
*K. A. Shahriar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: SPaRSe-TIME是一个结构化时间序列预测框架，将时间序列分解为显著性、记忆和趋势三个互补组件，通过投影到信息子空间实现高效、可解释的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列架构（如RNN和注意力机制）统一处理所有时间步，计算成本高，而真实时间信号通常具有异质结构，信息模式稀疏分布且夹杂冗余观测。

Method: 将时间序列分解为三个组件：显著性（数据依赖的稀疏化算子）、记忆（捕获主导低秩时间模式）、趋势（编码低频动态）。通过轻量级自适应映射集成这些组件，实现选择性、可解释的时间推理。

Result: 在多样化真实数据集上，SPaRSe-TIME相比循环和注意力架构实现了竞争性预测性能，同时显著降低计算复杂度。在具有清晰时间组件的结构化时间序列中特别有效，并通过组件贡献提供显式可解释性。

Conclusion: SPaRSe-TIME为单一序列模型提供了原则性替代方案，在效率、可解释性和性能之间架起桥梁，为时间序列学习提供了可扩展框架。分析揭示了基于分解建模的优势和局限性，特别是在高度随机和复杂多元设置中的挑战。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is traditionally dominated by sequence-based architectures such as recurrent neural networks and attention mechanisms, which process all time steps uniformly and often incur substantial computational cost. However, real-world temporal signals typically exhibit heterogeneous structure, where informative patterns are sparsely distributed and interspersed with redundant observations. This work introduces \textbf{SPaRSe-TIME}, a structured and computationally efficient framework that models time series through a decomposition into three complementary components: saliency, memory, and trend. The proposed approach reformulates temporal modeling as a projection onto informative subspaces, where saliency acts as a data-dependent sparsification operator, memory captures dominant low-rank temporal patterns, and trend encodes low-frequency dynamics. These components are integrated through a lightweight, adaptive mapping that enables simplified, selective, and interpretable temporal reasoning. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world datasets demonstrate that SPaRSe-TIME achieves competitive predictive performance compared to recurrent and attention-based architectures, while significantly reducing computational complexity. The model is particularly effective in structured time series with clear temporal components and provides explicit interpretability through component-wise contributions. Furthermore, analysis reveals both the strengths and limitations of decomposition-based modeling, highlighting challenges in highly stochastic and complex multivariate settings. Overall, SPaRSe-TIME offers a principled alternative to monolithic sequence models, bridging efficiency, interpretability, and performance, and providing a scalable framework for time series learning.

</details>


### [153] [A Novel 3D Antenna Architecture with Spatial Resource Allocation for Massive MIMO HAPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17533)
*Rozita Shafie,Omid Abbasi,Halim Yanikomeroglu,Mohammad Javad Omidi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合空间资源分配和用户分簇的方法来缓解大规模MIMO HAPS系统中的空间相关性挑战，通过正交信道增益用户共享资源块、非正交用户分配不同资源块，并采用扇区化天线架构来降低天线单元间的空间相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MIMO HAPS系统中天线单元间的固有空间相关性导致用户信道增益高度相关和干扰，这是系统性能的主要挑战。

Method: 1) 集成空间资源分配和用户分簇：将相同资源块分配给具有正交信道增益的用户，非正交信道增益用户获得不同资源块；2) 提出扇区化天线架构，直接向用户传输三维波束以降低天线单元间的空间相关性；3) 联合优化功率分配和资源分配问题以最大化系统总数据速率。

Result: 仿真结果表明空间资源分配在管理用户间空间相关性和干扰方面发挥了重要作用。

Conclusion: 所提出的集成方法有效缓解了大规模MIMO HAPS系统中的空间相关性挑战，通过合理的资源分配和天线架构设计提高了系统性能。

Abstract: Spatial correlation poses a significant challenge in massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) high-altitude platform station (HAPS) systems. The inherent spatial correlation among antenna elements on the HAPS induces high correlation and interference among users' channel gains. To mitigate this issue, we propose an integrated approach that combines spatial resource allocation and user clustering. In our proposed solution, we assign the same resource blocks to users with orthogonal channel gains, while users with non-orthogonal channel gains receive different resource blocks. Additionally, we propose a sectorized antenna architecture for the massive MIMO HAPS base station, specifically designed to directly transmit three-dimensional beams to users and reduce spatial correlation among antenna elements. This work addresses the joint optimization problem of power allocation and resource allocation to maximize the overall data rate of the massive MIMO HAPS system. Simulation results revealed the role of spatial resource allocation in managing spatial correlation and interference among users.

</details>


### [154] [Active MIMO Sensing With Exploration-Exploitation Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17582)
*Nadim Ghaddar,Kareem M. Attiah,Wei Yu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于MIMO雷达系统的主动感知框架，通过贝叶斯克拉美-罗界优化自适应设计发射和接收波束形成器，解决探索-利用权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的MIMO雷达波束形成设计通常采用固定或非自适应策略，无法充分利用先前感知阶段的信息。本文旨在开发一种自适应波束形成框架，通过优化贝叶斯克拉美-罗界来提升未知感知参数的估计性能，同时解决探索新方向与利用已知信息之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出基于BCRB优化的主动感知框架：1）在每个感知阶段基于先前测量自适应设计发射和接收波束形成器；2）通过拉格朗日对偶优化最小化BCRB；3）提出两种变体：探索中心型（确保探测多个正交波束方向）和利用中心型（不限制最优波束形成器数量）；4）采用交替优化算法分别求解发射和接收波束形成器；5）分析优化问题的全局最优性条件。

Result: 理论分析表明：1）交替优化算法在子问题全局最优解条件下收敛到稳定点；2）尽管优化问题非凸，但在特定特征值多重性条件下可保证全局最优性；3）这些条件意味着半定松弛的紧致性。仿真结果表明，相比现有自适应波束形成策略，所提BCRB基设计具有显著性能优势。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了一种基于BCRB优化的MIMO雷达自适应波束形成框架，有效解决了探索-利用权衡问题。理论分析证明了算法的收敛性和全局最优性条件，仿真验证了相比现有方法的性能提升。该框架为主动感知系统提供了系统化的波束形成设计方法。

Abstract: This paper develops an active sensing framework for designing the transmit and receive beamformers of a multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) radar system. In the proposed technique, the beamformers are adaptively designed in each sensing stage based on the measurements made in the previous sensing stages. The beamformers are determined by minimizing the Bayesian Cram{é}r-Rao bound (BCRB) for the estimation of the unknown sensing parameters at each stage via Lagrangian dual optimization. To address the exploration-exploitation tradeoff that is inherent to such an adaptive design, this paper proposes two variants of the BCRB optimization problem: an exploration-centric variant, that ensures that multiple orthogonal beamforming directions are probed in each sensing stage, and an exploitation-centric variant, that does not restrict the number of optimal beamformers. Each variant of the optimization problem is solved via an alternating optimization algorithm that alternates between solving for the transmit beamformers and solving for the receive beamformers. The algorithm is shown to converge to a stationary point provided that each optimization problem is solved to global optimality. Moreover, this paper studies each of the two BCRB optimization sub-problems in the Lagrangian dual domain and shows that despite the non-convexity, global optimality is guaranteed provided that certain sufficient conditions hold. The conditions pertain to the multiplicity of the eigenvalues of a specific direction matrix that can be analytically written in terms of the optimal dual variables. These conditions further imply the tightness of the semidefinite relaxation of the optimization problems. Simulation results demonstrate the benefits of the proposed BCRB-based design compared to state-of-the-art adaptive beamforming strategies.

</details>


### [155] [Conjugate Beamforming Variants for Multicasting in Cell-Free Massive MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17631)
*Alejandro de la Fuente,Adrián Espinosa,Jan García-Morales,Guillem Femenias,Felip Riera-Palou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无小区大规模MIMO系统中物理层多播的可扩展共轭波束赋形变体，分析了经典CB、归一化CB和增强CB在子群中心多播框架下的性能，发现NCB在大多数场景下提供稳健的性能-复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无小区大规模MIMO系统中支持宽带和多媒体服务的低复杂度分布式预编码器和多播传输模式的选择问题，解决大规模用户场景下的可扩展性和性能优化挑战。

Method: 采用基于大尺度衰落相似性的子群中心多播框架，将多播用户划分为子群以实现复合信道估计、导频重用和低复杂度分布式预编码。分析经典共轭波束赋形(CB)、归一化CB(NCB)和增强CB(ECB)三种变体，在均匀分布、空间聚类和异构场景等代表性用户几何下评估聚合频谱效率性能。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟显示性能具有强烈的空间几何依赖性：在均匀部署中单播传输更优，而在聚类和异构场景中子群多播变得至关重要。在CB基预编码器中，NCB在大多数场景下提供稳健的性能-复杂度权衡，而ECB仅在存在足够信道硬化时提供额外增益。

Conclusion: 研究结果为CF-mMIMO系统中支持宽带和多媒体服务的低复杂度分布式预编码器和多播传输模式选择提供了实用见解，强调了根据用户空间几何特性选择适当传输策略的重要性。

Abstract: This paper studies scalable conjugate beamforming (CB) variants for physical-layer multicasting in cell-free massive multiple-input multiple-output (CF-mMIMO) systems. Focusing on fully distributed precoding, we analyze classical CB, normalized CB (NCB), and enhanced CB (ECB) within a subgroup-centric multicast framework. Multicast users are partitioned into subgroups based on large-scale fading similarity, which enables composite channel estimation, pilot reuse, and distributed precoding with low complexity. The performance of the different CB variants is evaluated in terms of aggregated spectral efficiency (ASE) under representative user geometries, including uniformly distributed users, spatially clustered deployments, and heterogeneous scenarios combining hotspots with more dispersed users. Monte Carlo simulations reveal a strong spatial geometry-dependent behavior: unicast transmission is preferable in uniform deployments, while subgroup-based multicasting becomes essential in clustered and heterogeneous scenarios. Among the CB-based precoders, NCB offers a robust performance-complexity trade-off across most scenarios, whereas ECB provides additional gains only when sufficient channel hardening is present. These results provide practical insights into the selection of low-complexity distributed precoders and multicast transmission modes in CF-mMIMO systems supporting broadband and multimedia services.

</details>


### [156] [Symbol-Level Mask-Compliant Hybrid Precoding for Multi-User MIMO-OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17853)
*Navid Reyhanian,Parisa Ramezani,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种用于混合多用户MIMO-OFDM系统的鲁棒联合数字-RF预编码设计，在存在相位误差和频谱约束下最小化下行链路总均方误差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波MIMO系统需要混合预编码来平衡性能和硬件成本，但OFDM系统存在带外辐射问题，且非理想移相器引入相位误差，需要设计鲁棒的预编码方案

Method: 基于最小均方误差的块坐标下降算法，交替更新发射端数字-RF预编码器和用户端数字-RF组合器，每个子问题采用计算高效、可扩展的闭式解策略

Result: 广泛仿真验证了所提方法，相比现有基准方案显示出明显的性能提升

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒联合数字-RF预编码设计能够有效解决毫米波混合MIMO-OFDM系统中的相位误差和频谱约束问题，提供实用且性能优越的解决方案

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) technology is a crucial enabler for next-generation networks because it offers substantially greater available bandwidth. mmWave multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems cannot rely solely on fully digital precoding due to hardware costs. As a result, hybrid precoding, which combines digital baseband processing with RF precoding, has emerged as a practical solution that balances performance and implementation complexity. As mmWave links typically operate over wideband, frequency-selective channels, orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) is commonly used to mitigate dispersive effects, yet OFDM introduces practical drawbacks, including out-of-band (OOB) emissions from abrupt spectral transitions among subcarriers and additional spectral leakage induced by windowing. Moreover, nonideal phase shifters (PS) in the RF transmit precoder and the user combiner impose inherent implementation limits that result in phase errors. We investigate robust joint digital--RF precoder design for minimizing the downlink sum mean-squared error (MSE) in hybrid multi-user (MU) MIMO--OFDM systems subject to maximum transmit-power, clipping, and OOB spectral-mask constraints. The resulting optimization is nonconvex and challenging to solve. To address this, we develop a minimum mean-squared error (MMSE) based block coordinate descent (BCD) algorithm that alternates between updating the transmitter-side digital--RF precoders and the user-side digital--RF combiners. For each BCD subproblem, we propose computationally efficient and scalable, closed-form solution strategies suitable for practical implementation. Extensive simulations validate the proposed methods and show clear performance improvements over established benchmark schemes.

</details>


### [157] [Joint Phase Noise and Off-Grid Channel Estimation for AFDM Systems via Sparse Bayesian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17858)
*You Xu,Huaijin Zhang,Lixia Xiao,Guanghua Liu,Zilong Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于稀疏贝叶斯学习的联合相位噪声与信道估计方法，解决AFDM系统中相位噪声和分数偏移耦合导致的估计误差问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际AFDM系统中，振荡器相位噪声与分数偏移的复杂耦合导致传统估计器在高信噪比下出现严重的误差平台，需要新的联合估计方法

Method: 提出JPNCE-SBL方法：1) 引入降秩子空间投影捕获维纳相位噪声过程的主特征能量；2) 设计动态网格演化策略迭代消除离网误差；3) 在统一的EM框架中联合更新信道和相位噪声估计

Result: 仿真结果表明，JPNCE-SBL在NMSE和BER方面显著优于现有基准方法，在实用相位噪声条件下接近完美信道状态信息的性能

Conclusion: 提出的联合估计方法有效解决了AFDM系统中相位噪声和分数偏移的耦合问题，通过降秩投影和动态网格演化实现了高性能的联合估计

Abstract: In practical affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) systems, the intricate coupling of oscillator phase noise (PN) and off-grid fractional shifts traps conventional estimators in a severe high-SNR error floor. To address these challenges, we propose a joint PN and channel estimation method based on sparse Bayesian learning (JPNCE-SBL). Specifically, a reduced-rank subspace projection is first introduced to capture the dominant eigen-energy of the Wiener PN process. Concurrently, a dynamic grid evolution strategy is designed to iteratively eliminate off-grid errors without requiring computationally prohibitive global grid densification. Both components are integrated into a unified Expectation-Maximization (EM) framework, where the channel and PN estimates are jointly updated at each iteration to prevent error propagation. Simulation results demonstrate that JPNCE-SBL significantly outperforms existing benchmarks in both NMSE and BER, closely approaching the perfect channel state information case under practical PN conditions.

</details>


### [158] [Ray Tracing-Enabled Digital Twin for RIS Phase Optimization: Implementation and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17929)
*Ömer Lütfü Karakelle,Sefa Kayraklık,İbrahim Hökelek,Ali Görçin,Halim Yanıkömeroğlu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于数字孪生的RIS相位优化框架，利用射线追踪构建环境数字模型，通过收发端位置信息计算最优相位配置，避免传统高开销信道估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RIS相位优化需要复杂的信道估计过程，产生高导频开销，在时变无线环境中难以实时部署，成为实际应用瓶颈。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Sionna射线追踪库构建物理环境的三维地图数字孪生，利用收发端位置信息在数字孪生中计算最优RIS相位配置，然后将配置传输到物理RIS原型。

Result: 实验结果表明，从数字孪生获得的相位配置能显著增强物理环境中的接收信号功率，验证了射线追踪模型的保真度和所提优化策略的可行性。

Conclusion: 数字孪生驱动的RIS相位优化框架能够有效避免高维信道估计带来的信令开销，为时变无线环境中RIS的实时部署提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Determining the optimal phase configurations of reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) elements typically requires complex channel estimation procedures with high pilot overhead, creating a bottleneck for real-time deployment in time-varying wireless environments. In this paper, we propose a digital twin (DT)-driven framework for RIS phase shift optimization that eliminates extensive signaling overhead associated with estimating high-dimensional RIS channels. Leveraging the NVIDIA Sionna ray-tracing library, we construct a DT of the physical environment based on a three-dimensional map. The proposed system utilizes the location information of the transceivers to compute the optimal RIS phase shift configurations within the DT. These computationally generated configurations are then transferred to a physical RIS prototype. Experimental results demonstrate that the phase configurations obtained from the DT significantly enhance the received signal power in the physical environment, validating the fidelity of the ray-tracing model and the feasibility of the proposed optimization strategy.

</details>


### [159] [Paradigm Shift from Statistical Channel Modeling to Digital Twin Prediction: An Environment-Generalizable ChannelLM for 6G AI-enabled Air Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18021)
*Yichen Cai,Yuelong Qiu,Jianhua Zhang,Li Yu,Yuxiang Zhang,Zhen Zhang,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信道大模型（ChannelLM）的数字孪生信道架构，通过环境重建、特征提取和大模型映射，在未见测试环境中实现了比小规模AI模型低4.23dB的预测误差和70毫秒的端到端推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G发展对无线信道获取提出了更高要求，传统统计信道建模依赖有限环境的离线测量数据，难以支持面向多样化环境的在线应用。现有AI模型在实现强环境泛化能力和端到端实时信道预测方面存在困难。

Method: 提出ChannelLM驱动的DTC架构，包含三个模块：1）基于动态物体检测和图像-点云多模态对齐的低复杂度高精度环境重建；2）物理可解释的环境特征提取；3）ChannelLM核心将特征映射为广义环境表示，用于多任务信道预测。

Result: 在未见测试环境中，相比小规模AI模型，ChannelLM将信道状态信息预测误差降低了4.23dB，同时在真实世界中实现了70毫秒的端到端推理延迟。

Conclusion: 提出的ChannelLM驱动的DTC架构能够有效解决现有AI模型在环境泛化和实时端到端信道预测方面的局限性，为6G时代的无线信道获取提供了创新解决方案。

Abstract: As 6G advances, ubiquitous connectivity and higher capacity requirements of the air interface pose substantial challenges for accurate and real-time wireless channel acquisition in diverse environments. Conventional statistical channel modeling relies on offline measurement data from limited environments, struggling to support online applications facing diverse environments. To this end, the digital twin channel (DTC) has emerged as a novel paradigm that constructs a digital replica of the physical environment through high-fidelity sensing and predicts corresponding channel in real time utilizing artificial intelligence (AI) models. As the engine of DTC, existing AI models struggle to simultaneously achieve strong environmental generalization in real-world and end-to-end channel prediction for real time tasks. Therefore, this paper proposes a channel large model (ChannelLM)-driven DTC architecture comprising three modules: low-complexity and high-accuracy environment reconstruction based on dynamic object detection and multimodal alignment of image and point cloud data, physically interpretable environment feature extraction, and a ChannelLM core to mapping these features into generalized environment representations for multi-task channel prediction. Simulation results demonstrate that, in unseen test environments, compared with small-scale AI models, ChannelLM reduces prediction errors by 4.23 dB in channel state information prediction while achieving an end-to-end inference latency of 70 milliseconds in the real world.

</details>


### [160] [User Mobility Demands Near-Field Communications in Terahertz Band Wireless Networks Beyond 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18040)
*Peng Zhang,Vitaly Petrov,Arjun Singh,Emil Björnson,Josep Miquel Jornet*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文通过矛盾证明框架分析太赫兹移动链路能否在纯辐射远场条件下保持宽带性能，发现静止链路可行而移动链路不可行，需要近场感知设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹频率下的大孔径阵列增益需求常导致近场传播不可避免，但近场操作使移动系统设计复杂化。本文旨在探究移动太赫兹链路能否在纯辐射远场条件下保持宽带、实现高数据率和覆盖范围。

Method: 开发矛盾证明可行性框架，联合强制执行基于夫琅禾费距离的远场要求和由最差链路距离处目标信噪比定义的可靠性要求。推导静止和移动链路的远场可行带宽闭式上界，并纳入多种用户设备旋转和移动场景的实际失准情况。

Result: 数值结果表明：静止太赫兹链路可通过物理可实现孔径保持纯远场操作并支持极大带宽，而实际移动太赫兹系统则无法实现。在相关移动接入场景中，远场可行带宽成为严重限制因素：实现数十GHz目标需要不切实际的高用户设备发射功率。跨频段比较显示，sub-6GHz和中等带宽的毫米波可基本实现纯远场操作，而移动太赫兹接入需要近场感知设计。

Conclusion: 移动太赫兹系统无法在纯辐射远场条件下保持宽带性能，远场可行带宽成为严重瓶颈。与sub-6GHz和毫米波不同，移动太赫兹接入需要近场感知设计方法。

Abstract: Near-field propagation is often unavoidable at terahertz (THz) frequencies due to the large apertures needed for sufficient array gain, yet near-field operation complicates practical system design, especially under user mobility. This paper asks whether a mobile THz link can remain broadband, achieve the desired high rates and coverage, while operating exclusively in the radiative far field. To answer this question, we develop a proof-by-contradiction feasibility framework that jointly enforces (i) a far-field requirement based on the Fraunhofer distance and (ii) a reliability requirement specified by a target SNR at the worst-case link distance. We derive closed-form upper bounds on the far-field-feasible bandwidth for stationary and mobile links. We further incorporate practical misalignment through several UE rotation and mobility scenarios. Numerical results show that stationary THz links can remain far-field-only with physically realizable apertures while supporting extremely large bandwidths, whereas practical mobile THz systems cannot. In practically relevant mobile THz access settings, the far-field-feasible bandwidth becomes a severe limiting factor: achieving tens-of-GHz targets would require unrealistically high UE transmit power. A cross-band comparison further shows that far-field-only operation is largely attainable at sub-6~GHz and, to a significant extent, at mmWave for moderate bandwidths, while near-field-aware designs become essential for mobile THz access.

</details>


### [161] [Low-Complexity Tone Injection via Candidate Ranking for PAPR Reduction in OFDM and AFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18060)
*Yupeng Zheng,Ang Li,Jinfei Wang,Yi Ma,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于候选排序和深度优先搜索的新型音调注入方案，在OFDM和AFDM系统中实现超过1dB的PAPR性能提升，同时保持与FFT相当的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于随机候选生成或削波噪声频谱的音调注入方案在PAPR性能上存在根本性限制，需要开发更有效的TI方案来改善性能-复杂度权衡。

Method: 提出两种新型TI方案：1）通过时域局部峰值引导的候选排序程序迭代更新TI序列；2）进一步集成深度优先搜索以利用过程的树状结构增强PAPR性能。两种方案都与OFDM和AFDM系统兼容。

Result: 仿真表明，在可比复杂度下，所提方案相比基准TI方案实现了超过1dB的PAPR增益。该增益在不同子载波数量下保持一致，证实了在OFDM和AFDM系统中优越的性能-复杂度权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于候选排序和深度优先搜索的音调注入方案显著提升了PAPR性能，同时保持了与FFT相当的复杂度，为OFDM和AFDM系统提供了有效的无失真PAPR降低解决方案。

Abstract: Tone injection (TI) is a promising distortionless PAPR reduction technique that incurs no spectral efficiency loss. However, state-of-the-art TI schemes based on random candidate generation or clipping noise spectrum suffer from fundamental limitations in PAPR performance. In this paper, we propose novel TI schemes compatible with both OFDM and AFDM systems. The proposed schemes iteratively update the TI sequence via a candidate ranking procedure guided by time-domain local peaks. This accurately selects effective candidates while achieving a complexity comparable to that of the fast Fourier transform. Depth-first search is further integrated to enhance PAPR performance by exploiting the tree structure of the process. Simulations demonstrate that the proposed schemes achieve over 1 dB PAPR gain over baseline TI schemes at comparable complexity. The gain is consistent across various numbers of subcarriers under controlled per-iteration complexities, confirming a superior performance-complexity trade-off for both OFDM and AFDM.

</details>


### [162] [Cramér-Rao Bound Optimization for Near-Field ISAC with Extended Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18166)
*Zongyao Zhao,Zhaolin Wang,Lincong Han,Liang Xu,Jing Jin,Yuanwei Liu,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对扩展目标的近场ISAC几何感知发射设计方法，通过参数化建模、CRB推导和降维SDR优化，相比点目标和几何无关基线实现了更低的CRB值和计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近场ISAC中，当目标具有不可忽略的空间扩展时，点目标模型不再适用。需要开发能够处理扩展目标几何特性的传输设计方法，以提高参数估计精度。

Method: 1. 使用中心、方向和尺寸参数对扩展目标进行几何建模；2. 在球形波传播下推导几何参数的CRB；3. 识别精确的ET感知降维子空间；4. 在SINR和功率约束下开发降维半定松弛(SDR)优化方法。

Result: 仿真结果显示：1. 相比点目标和几何无关基线，提出的方法获得了更低的CRB值；2. 对于大规模阵列，计算运行时间显著减少。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了针对扩展目标的几何感知传输设计在近场ISAC中的有效性，通过参数化建模和降维优化，同时提高了参数估计精度和计算效率。

Abstract: Near-field integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) requires target models beyond the point-target abstraction when the target has a non-negligible spatial extent. In this letter, a geometry-aware transmit design is developed for a parametric extended target (ET) described by its center, orientation, and size under spherical-wave propagation. The CRB for the geometric parameters is formulated around a nominal ET state, an exact ET-aware reduced subspace is identified for the lifted covariance formulation, and a reduced-dimensional semidefinite relaxation (SDR) is developed under signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) and power constraints. Simulation results show lower CRB values than point-target and geometry-agnostic baselines together with substantially reduced runtime for large arrays.

</details>


### [163] [Passive RIS Is Not Silent: Revisiting Performance Limits Under Thermal Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18263)
*Farjam Karim,Deepak Kumar,Prathapasinghe Dharmawansa,Nurul Huda Mahmood,Arthur Sousa de Sena,Matti-Latva-aho*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 重新审视被动RIS无噪声的假设，提出考虑RIS热噪声的分析框架，证明忽略该噪声会导致性能评估失真


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动RIS通常被认为无噪声，但这种理想化假设可能导致误导性的性能评估。需要重新审视RIS热噪声对系统性能的实际影响

Method: 提出可处理的近似分析框架，将RIS引起的热噪声纳入系统模型，推导关键性能指标（中断概率、吞吐量）的闭式表达式

Result: 仿真验证了近似分析的有效性，显示忽略RIS热噪声会导致显著的性能差异，揭示了接收机噪声与RIS噪声之间的权衡关系

Conclusion: 被动RIS的热噪声不可忽视，对6G通信系统的鲁棒高效设计具有重要指导意义

Abstract: Reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) have emerged as a promising solution for enabling energy-efficient and flexible spectrum usage in wireless communication, particularly in the context of sixth-generation (6G) networks. While passive RIS architectures are widely regarded as virtually noiseless due to the lack of active components, this idealized assumption can lead to misleading performance evaluations. In this paper, we revisit this assumption and demonstrate that the thermal noise generated by passive RIS elements, though often neglected, can significantly affect system performance. We propose a tractable approximated analytical framework that incorporates RIS-induced thermal noise into the system and derive closed-form expressions for key performance metrics, such as outage probability and throughput. Simulation results validate our approximated analysis and highlight the substantial performance discrepancies that arise when RIS thermal noise is ignored.
  Our results offer valuable insights into the trade-offs between receiver and RIS noise, guiding the development of robust and efficient 6G communication systems.

</details>


### [164] [Warm-Start Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm for QAM MIMO Data Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18479)
*Soumyadip Paul,Sourav Banerjee,Debanjan Bhowmik,Neel Kanth Kundu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于大规模MIMO系统中高阶QAM数据检测的混合量子-经典检测框架，采用基于低秩半定松弛的预热启动线性斜坡量子近似优化算法（WSLR-QAOA）来解决高阶无约束二进制优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模MIMO系统采用高阶QAM调制时，经典最大似然检测器面临指数级复杂度挑战。格雷编码调制引入非线性符号到比特映射，将问题转化为高阶无约束二进制优化问题，进一步增加了检测难度。

Method: 提出混合量子-经典检测框架：1）基于低秩半定松弛的结构化预热启动，采用块坐标下降法求解；2）线性斜坡参数化的量子近似优化算法（WSLR-QAOA）用于求解高阶无约束二进制优化问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明：1）该框架在符号错误率方面优于经典方法；2）比标准QAOA收敛更快；3）性能接近最优ML检测器；4）在IBM量子硬件上验证，低信噪比下达到近ML性能，高信噪比下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于高阶无约束二进制优化的WSLR-QAOA算法在大规模MIMO数据检测中具有实际应用潜力，通过预热启动和线性斜坡参数化显著提升了量子优化算法的性能和收敛速度。

Abstract: Data detection in large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems with higher-order quadrature amplitude modulation (QAM) remains a challenging problem due to the exponential complexity of the classical maximum likelihood (ML) detector. This challenge is further amplified by Gray-coded modulation, which introduces nonlinear symbol-to-bit mappings and transforms the problem into a higher-order unconstrained binary optimization (HUBO) formulation. To address this problem, this paper presents a hybrid quantum-classical detection framework that leverages a warm-start linear-ramp Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (WSLR-QAOA) for solving the resulting HUBO problem. A structured warm-start based on a low-rank semidefinite relaxation, solved via a block coordinate descent (BCD) method, provides an efficient and high-quality initialization, while a linear ramp parameterization guides the QAOA optimization. Simulation results show that the proposed framework outperforms classical methods in terms of symbol error rate (SER) and converges faster than standard QAOA, while achieving performance close to the optimal ML detector. Furthermore, the WSLR-QAOA algorithm is validated on actual IBM quantum hardware, where it achieves near-ML performance at low SNR and maintains competitive accuracy at higher SNR despite moderate degradation due to hardware noise. This demonstrates the practical potential of the HUBO-based WSLR-QAOA algorithm for large-scale MIMO data detection.

</details>


### [165] [Joint Scheduling of Multi-Band Radar Sensing and DNN Inference for Cross-Stage Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18520)
*Yanan Du,Sai Xu,Kezhi Wang,Yansha Deng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出联合调度框架，通过跨阶段并行减少多波段雷达感知与DNN推理的端到端延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阶段式设计将雷达感知和DNN推理视为两个顺序阶段，导致延迟增加。需要利用跨阶段并行性，允许感知完成的波段立即开始推理，无需等待所有波段完成

Method: 1) 建立联合调度问题，耦合感知时间分配、分支释放时机和非抢占式多核DAG执行；2) 开发释放感知启发式算法，评估感知决策对DAG完工时间的影响；3) 设计贪婪列表调度器处理多核DAG执行

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提设计能有效利用跨阶段并行性，在多种异构感知场景中相对于解耦基线显著降低端到端延迟，同时明确了延迟增益受限的操作区间

Conclusion: 通过联合调度雷达感知和DNN推理，利用跨阶段并行性可显著降低端到端延迟，为实时感知-推理系统提供有效解决方案

Abstract: This paper studies end-to-end latency minimization for a multi-band radar sensing and deep neural network (DNN) inference pipeline. Unlike conventional stage-wise designs that treat radar sensing and DNN inference as two sequential stages, the proposed framework exploits cross-stage parallelism by allowing the inference branch associated with a sensed band to start as soon as that band completes sensing, without waiting for all bands to finish. To characterize this interaction, we formulate a joint scheduling problem that couples sensing-time allocation, branch release timing, and non-preemptive multi-core execution of a directed acyclic graph (DAG) under sensing-feasibility, precedence, and core-capacity constraints. Since the resulting problem is combinatorial and strongly time-coupled, we further develop a release-aware heuristic that evaluates each sensing decision according to its downstream impact on the DAG makespan, together with a greedy list scheduler for multi-core DAG execution under release times. Simulation results show that the proposed design can effectively exploit cross-stage parallelism and reduce end-to-end latency relative to a decoupled baseline in many heterogeneous sensing scenarios, while also clarifying the operating regimes in which the latency gain becomes limited.

</details>

### [166] [Safety-Aware AoI Scheduling for LEO Satellite-Assisted Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17281)
*Kangkang Sun,Junyi He,Juntong Liu,Xiuzhen Chen,Jianhua Li,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: SafeScale-MATD3框架通过双时间尺度AoI模型和分层安全约束，解决了LEO卫星回程中车辆编队面临的复合多普勒、子时隙切换中断和异构新鲜度需求问题，显著降低了碰撞告警违规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架无法同时处理卫星和车辆运动带来的复合多普勒效应、超过碰撞告警时限的子时隙切换中断，以及不同车辆优先级类别的异构新鲜度需求。核心挑战在于时间尺度不匹配：粗粒度控制时隙隐藏了子时隙中断，使得AoI尖峰分析和安全验证变得困难。

Method: 提出统一框架，将双时间尺度AoI模型与通过虚拟队列强制执行的分层时间平均安全约束相结合。推导出乒乓切换的AoI包络闭式解，表明累积惩罚随振荡长度呈二次增长。基于漂移加惩罚模板实例化为SafeScale-MATD3，采用主动切换定时和多任务双评论家多智能体强化学习。

Result: SafeScale-MATD3是唯一满足严格1%碰撞告警违规预算的方法，相比基线将违规率降低了4-5.5倍，同时实现碰撞告警AoI降低35%，并在能量和新鲜度权衡上达到严格的帕累托最优。

Conclusion: 抑制短暂但重复的乒乓切换振荡比缩短任何单次中断带来更大的安全回报，时隙级AoI核算是LEO切换下可验证碰撞告警保证的必要条件。该框架为卫星辅助车辆编队提供了可验证的安全保障。

Abstract: Autonomous platoons traversing infrastructure gaps increasingly depend on LEO satellite backhaul for safety-critical updates, yet no existing framework jointly addresses compound Doppler from simultaneous satellite and vehicle motion, sub-slot handover outages that exceed collision-alert deadlines, and heterogeneous freshness requirements across three vehicular priority classes. The core challenge is a \emph{timescale mismatch}: coarse control slots hide sub-slot outages, which makes both AoI spike analysis and safety verification ill-posed. Ping-pong handover oscillations further compound AoI cost in a way that purely reactive schedulers cannot mitigate. We address these challenges through a unified framework that couples a two-timescale AoI model with tiered time-average safety constraints enforced by virtual queues. A closed-form ping-pong AoI envelope reveals that cumulative penalty grows quadratically in oscillation length, analytically justifying oscillation suppression as the highest-leverage safety mechanism. The resulting drift-plus-penalty template is instantiated as SafeScale-MATD3 with proactive handover timing and multi-task dual-critic MARL. A key finding is that suppressing brief but repeated ping-pong oscillations yields larger safety returns than shortening any single outage, and that tick-level AoI accounting is a necessary condition for verifiable collision-alert guarantees under LEO handovers. Simulations show that SafeScale-MATD3 is the only method satisfying the strict 1 % collision-alert violation budget, reducing violation rate by 4 to 5.5 times versus baselines, while achieving 35 % lower collision-alert AoI and strict Pareto dominance on the energy and freshness tradeoff.

</details>


### [167] [Spectrum Configuration Framework for Throughput Maximization in Open Systems with Roll-Off-Based QoT Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18374)
*Peyman Pahlevanzadeh,Venkata Virajit Garbhapu,Agastya Raj,Dmitrii Briantcev,Dan Kilper,Marco Ruffini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出用于开放解耦光系统的频谱配置框架，在保证传输质量余量的同时最大化吞吐量，通过联合优化收发器参数和WSS带宽，并采用背包优化算法实现高效频谱利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放解耦光系统中，需要在固定频谱分配约束下最大化吞吐量同时保证传输质量余量，这需要联合优化多个收发器参数和WSS配置。

Method: 提出频谱配置框架，联合优化调制格式、符号率、脉冲整形滚降因子和WSS带宽；首先实验评估滚降因子优化在级联WSS滤波下的影响，然后在OSaaS场景中应用基于背包问题的优化算法选择服务配置。

Result: 实验验证表明滚降因子优化在单通道和多通道传输中都能带来可测量的QoT增益；在城域规模开放测试平台上验证了所提方法能实现高效频谱利用和自适应吞吐量-余量权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的频谱配置框架能有效优化开放解耦光系统的性能，在保证传输质量的同时最大化吞吐量，为OSaaS等应用场景提供了实用的优化解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a spectrum-configuration framework for open and disaggregated optical systems that maximizes throughput while guaranteeing the quality of transmission (QoT) margins. The framework jointly optimizes transceiver parameters, including modulation format, symbol rate, pulse-shaping roll-off factor, and wavelength-selective switch (WSS) bandwidth, under fixed spectral allocation constraints. The impact of roll-off factor optimization is first experimentally evaluated in the presence of cascaded WSS filtering, demonstrating measurable QoT gains for both single- and multi-channel transmission. Building on these observations, a knapsack-based optimization is applied in the context of Optical Spectrum as a Service (OSaaS) to select service configurations that maximize aggregate throughput within a fixed spectrum width and limited transceiver resources. Experimental validation on a metro-scale open testbed confirms the effectiveness of the proposed approach in achieving efficient spectrum utilization and adaptive throughput-margin trade-offs.

</details>
